# Experiment ledger

Running record of what has been tried, what it gave, and what is still open. Update this file whenever an
experiment finishes or a decision is made. Last updated 2026-10-10.

**How results are measured**
- **Steven fold 0:** macro AUC on the fixed 825 fold-0 studies against Steven-v4 report labels, using only confident cells
  (≤ 0.3 or ≥ 0.7, positive cell weight). The trio/quad, max-confidence and cosine/EMA comparisons have shown direction agreement with public scoring; these selected examples are not a general predictive guarantee.
- **HPO fold 0:** the same 825 studies scored against HPO-v3.1-D2, with its own probabilities and confidence mask. Report this separately from Steven AUC. AUC near 0.95 on HPO is not comparable numerically to AUC near 0.90 on Steven. Some runs use HPO for controls and Steven only as an audit.
- **Steven-v4 is itself an LLM reading of the reports.** Label changes that move toward the host's image-based
  definitions can look worse on fold 0, so label experiments need a leaderboard check.
- **Gold:** 58 official-label studies, excluded from current report-trained comparison runs and label construction. Repeated target-level examination makes their later scores exploratory development evidence. Earlier archived fine-tuning experiments did train/select on the 29-study development half; they are listed below and must not be described as gold-unseen models.
- **LB:** Kaggle public leaderboard.
- **Uncertainty:** paired study bootstraps hold the trained checkpoints fixed. They do not include seed/platform variance or optimism from repeatedly selecting checkpoints and ensemble candidates on fold 0. Most experiments have one run per arm; a small gain does not isolate its cause.

## Leaderboard submissions

Selected attributable results, not a complete submission inventory. Official CLI history checked on 2026-10-10 returned **44 account entries: 37 with a public score, 7 without an exposed score**. The full inventory, including unattributed entries and original descriptions, is in `data/experiment_ledger_review_2026-10-10/submission_inventory.md`; raw receipt is `submissions.json`. Blank scores are not assumed to be successful scored models. Checkpoint provenance takes precedence over a submission's free-text description.

| Train/validate on v3.1 labels | Validation labels switched to v3.1; Steven logged only | 224px best epoch 18: HPO 0.946524 / Steven 0.891099; LB 0.924 | Tested; HPO controls also used in 336px and later local+global runs. No single-model LB gain established from the validation switch | `docs/hpo_v31_trainval_resnet50_experiment.md`; `kaggle/submission_kernels/hpo_v31_trainval_resnet50_submission/README.md` |
| Report-vs-gold audit | Readers scored on gold | Report labels ≈ 0.87–0.88 vs gold; report + image combined +0.030 [+0.010, +0.052] on gold | Reports and images make complementary errors on this small sample; this is not a measured theoretical text ceiling or a forecast for label refinement | `docs/image_detail_and_gold_ceiling_audit.md` |

### Training recipe

| Experiment | Change | Result | Verdict | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| AUC-margin loss | BCE + AUC-margin refinement from the HPO-v1 checkpoint | 0.8902 vs 0.8909 | No gain; margins were already met | `docs/hpo_v1_resnet50_auc_margin_results.md` |
| Pairwise logistic ranking from ImageNet | Steven-v4 r34 BCE + 0.1 macro confident positive/negative pair loss | Best fold 0 0.877167; LB 0.906, same as BCE-only submission | Distinct from the later AUC-saddle warm-start; this version did not improve rounded LB | `../cnn_topk_experiment/docs/auc_pairwise_loss_experiment.md`; `../data/kaggle_cli/cnn_pairwise_auc_fold0/`; submission 56699283 |
| Warm-start refinement | Continue training from the best checkpoint | See doc | No gain | `docs/hpo_resnet50_warmstart_refinement.md` |
| Cosine + EMA | Warm-up + cosine, 20 epochs, EMA 0.999 | Fold 0 0.8898, LB 0.914 | This combined schedule/EMA recipe underperformed; does not rule out other schedules or isolate EMA | `data/r50_cosine_ema_seed2026/` |
| AUC-based plateau controls | HPO-v1 +281 r34; both LR and early stopping use AUC | Best fold 0 0.885880 at epoch 22; LB 0.910 vs baseline 0.918 | Already tried; do not re-propose as new. AUC-LR/BCE-stop combination remains a separate untested idea | `data/hpo_v1_blend_auc_control_fold0/artifacts/run_progress.json`; submission 56749022 |
| Gold head-only / layer4+head fine-tuning | Report-trained r34; 29 development gold / 29 evaluation gold | Development OOF changes −0.0015 / −0.0060; evaluation changes +0.0018 / −0.0007 | No supported upgrade; these models used gold and are excluded from gold-unseen comparisons | `../cnn_topk_experiment/docs/gold_finetuning_results.md` |
| Gold preference fine-tuning | Frozen trunk, reference-relative pair loss + 0.1 BCE; head updates selected on development-gold OOF | 0.86525 vs baseline 0.86565 (−0.00040) | DPO-inspired classification objective, not literal LLM DPO; no supported upgrade | `../cnn_topk_experiment/docs/gold_dpo_experiment.md` |
| Batch16 / two-T4 ImageNet r50 | Global batch16, DataParallel splits slice windows | GPU memory preflight passed (~8.5–8.8 GiB allocated/GPU); run launched | Final result not reconciled here. Batch, BatchNorm statistics, optimizer-step count/warmup and platform must be documented before calling it a batch-only effect | `kaggle/experiment_kernels/hpo_v1_blend_resnet50_batch16_t4x2/`; saved user logs |

### Ensembling and checkpoints

| Experiment | Change | Result | Verdict | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |

## Not tried yet

Provisional priorities, not measured rankings by gain per compute. All numerical gain forecasts below are guesses, not confidence intervals or predictions established by these experiments. Measure throughput before quoting a GPU-hour budget: the original 224 px r50 D2 run took 22,781 seconds (~6.3h) for 24 epochs on Kaggle, so 3h/full run or 6–7h for two cross-fits plus retraining is not verified. **Rule** distinguishes recorded project constraints from competition eligibility, which must be checked separately; an internal preference is not a competition prohibition.

| # | Idea | Why | Expected gain | Cost | Rule | Status |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | **Test-time augmentation** on existing checkpoints: small in-plane shifts and zooms, no horizontal flips | Test whether local evidence is sensitive to sampling position; a lumbar result motivates the test but does not show it will lift every knee model | +0.002 to +0.005 (guess) | 1 Kaggle GPU run on fold 0, no training; benchmark multipass cost | OK | **Done 2026-10-10: passes for single models, no gain for the quad.** Kernel `tumis001/rsna-r50-tta-fold0` (T4, 1,500 s for 2 models × 11 views; parity 0.00048), output `data/r50_tta/kaggle_output/`, scorer `scripts/audit_fold0_tta.py`. Shift TTA (±0.06 grid): d2_ep15 0.8963 → tta5 0.8991 / tta9 0.8996 (+0.0033 [+0.0013, +0.0053]); hpo_v1_ep17 0.8909 → tta5 0.8942 / tta9 0.8948 (+0.0039 [+0.0018, +0.0061]); ≥ 99.9% of bootstraps better, gains on 11/12 targets (fracture −0.004 on d2). Intensity TTA (int3): +0.0001–0.0002, no effect. Quad with both members TTA'd: +0.0002 [−0.0007, +0.0010]; TTA is a form of averaging that the ensemble already provides. Use tta5 or tta9 for any single-model submission or new single model; skip it for the quad |
| 2 | **Image model as an extra reader** (Dawid–Skene with cross-fitted image predictions) | Report/image complementarity on gold motivates a hypothesis; +0.030 is for combining them at evaluation, not a demonstrated gain from training-label fusion | +0.005 to +0.015 (guess) | 2 half-split runs + 1 retrain; 6–7 GPU-h estimate unverified, 1 submission | **Needs recorded project-rule decision** | Awaiting decision. All predictions used as independent evidence must exclude the study and its duplicate/report group from teacher training; report/image readers are correlated, so Dawid–Skene independence is not assumed |
| 3 | **Cross-fitted label cleaning or distillation:** down-weight cells where held-out predictions strongly disagree, or blend report and image targets | Test disagreement handling without treating the model's confidence as proof that a report is wrong | Unknown; prior range is speculative | Same cross-fitted runs as #2 | **Needs recorded project-rule decision** | Awaiting decision. Do not automatically erase rare positives or infer a diagnostic negative from silence; 0.5/0.5 is a candidate mixing choice, not a proven correction |
| 4 | **OrthoFoundation frozen-feature test:** proposed knee-MRI-pretrained ViT-L; small top-k head vs frozen ImageNet DINOv3-L | Adds knee-specific pretraining, whereas ImageNet and RadImageNet already supplied outside pretraining | Unknown | ~2 GPU-h estimate unverified; full fine-tune much heavier | Verify model provenance/licence and external-weights eligibility | **Frozen part done by the teammate** (2026-10-10, see the architecture table): Steven fold 0 0.851, below the r50 on every target; ensemble +0.0006. Still untested: a frozen ImageNet DINOv3-L control with the same head (to separate knee pretraining from ViT-L capacity) and a fine-tune (~15× r50 FLOPs per image at 224 px; budget not measured). GitHub repo states MIT licence; competition external-weights eligibility not checked |
| 5 | **All-report-data retrain** of the 0.925 recipe for a preselected duration; optionally separate gold-inclusive final fit | 4,349 vs 3,524 report-training studies, ~23% more | +0.002 to +0.008 (guess) | 1 run; 3h estimate unverified; no independent fold 0 score once its 825 studies enter training | Gold-inclusive fit is a separate decision | Proposed. Original best checkpoint index 15 means **16 completed epochs**, not 15. Define epoch vs optimizer-step budget and warmup explicitly before removing validation; never use the consumed fold as held-out evidence |
| 6 | **5-member ensemble:** quad + local_global_hpo | Fold 0 +0.0011 [+0.0001, +0.0022] | ~+0.001 | New inference notebook (own model code and slice layout), 1 submission | OK | Proposed |
| 7 | **Synovitis silence as unknown** (positive-unlabelled style loss) | Report evidence is sparse and inferred labels track Effusion; gold prevalence is from a small enriched sample, not a training-prior estimate | Unknown; ~+0.004 was a speculative upper-sized gain | 1 run + 1 submission | OK | Proposed. The 0.25× inferred-cell weighting experiment already failed; a mask/PU objective is distinct. Do not fit a PU class prior to gold or assume every silent report is negative |
| 8 | **Ordinal severity targets** per finding | Avoids guessing the host's cut-off; current soft labels already order severity, so the gain is limited | +0.001 to +0.003 | 1 run | OK | Low priority |
| 9 | 5-fold models + tree/boosting stacker on CNN features and metadata | Our logistic stacker lost −0.008; trees would overfit more | +0.001 to +0.003 at best | 4 extra runs | OK | Low priority |
| 10 | Target-specific series or crops (radiologist's view per finding) | Lumbar 2nd place used axial T2 for spinal stenosis | Unknown | Needs localization we don't have | OK | Parked |
| 11 | Larger offline LLM re-extraction of weak targets | Existing readers reach ~0.88 on the small gold sample; this is not a theoretical ceiling | Unknown | Needs approved GPU compute; Kaggle/Modal feasibility to measure | Hosted report transfer is excluded by the recorded current project constraints | Parked; no host GPU does not by itself rule out offline Kaggle inference |

### Additional proposals and controls from this review

These are not new launches. They complement the other agent's pending TTA test and do not change a running experiment.

| Idea | What remains untested / why it matters | First step / cost | Acceptance and constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Series-selection quality audit and repair** | Sampled inputs showed possible plane/FS misclassification. The failed sequence-conditioned logit correction did not fix which acquisition was selected | Kaggle CPU: audit DICOM orientation and available FS metadata against each selected slot; missing FS tags stay unknown. Quantify replacement/missing-slot counts before rebuilding caches | Test verified slot-selection changes with the same split, labels and recipe. Correct orientation is not established by description strings alone; no claim of AUC gain before training |
| **Re-examine the existing spatial-attention experiment** | Target-query spatial pooling before CNN averaging was already implemented in the teammate's ResNet34; this is not an untried architecture idea | CPU inspection of the actual trained config, checkpoint gates and gradient paths; distinguish enabled code from unused optional modules | A clean matched attention-only repeat remains unisolated, but requires a concrete explanation for why it should improve on the prior attempt. Do not re-propose the existing architecture as new |
| **Repeat-seed / independent-fold verification** | One seed per variant cannot separate a small recipe gain from training variance; repeated search on 825 studies also biases candidate selection | One matched seed repeat, or a predeclared additional report fold if affordable; preserve report/duplicate groups | A measurement control, not a guaranteed improvement. Record platform, batch/BN, optimizer steps and selection reference; bootstrap intervals alone are insufficient |
| **Distill the 0.937 quad into one reader** | Compression could preserve ensemble information with lower submission cost; it is not automatically a new AUC gain | CPU design first; use OOF teacher scores if changing labels, or explicitly document in-sample teacher use as distillation rather than independent evidence | Depends on the recorded project decision for model-derived training targets. No test/gold-derived fitting. Compare student AUC, ensemble contribution and inference cost, not just teacher-matching loss |
| **Window sampling / pooling allocation** | Uniform sampling of 24 out of 48 or 80 windows can dilute rare focal evidence. No isolated comparison yet of per-series quotas, depth-stratified coverage, or target-aware pooling | CPU coverage audit first; select one sampler/pooling hypothesis rather than adding several at once | Keep evaluation coverage and validation reference fixed. Sampling must not use held-out predictions/labels to choose training windows; extra windows are not assumed beneficial |
| **AUC-LR with BCE early stopping** | User proposed this combination earlier. The existing AUC-control run used AUC for both; it is not this test | Training only if evidence justifies cost; predeclare the exact stopping rule and checkpoint metric | Already-tried AUC/AUC and BCE/AUC controls remain separate records. BCE stopping can end while AUC improves, so this is not a preferred next run without a specific hypothesis |

Before spending GPU time on new candidates, reconcile the final batch-16/RadImageNet artifacts and obtain saved validation predictions for the salvaged ResNet34 if it is to be screened. Evaluate additions and swaps against the **current 0.937 quad**, not only against the 0.925 single model. The HPO-selected local+global r50 add/swap audit already exists and should not be repeated as though new.

### Beyond-0.95 single-model programme (2026-10-10)

The context is the public 0.95x notebooks (hitarthjain0 v5, rainycloudss v10, rabari9999 0.95 / 0.954), pulled to `data/public_notebooks/`. All four are one lineage built on one CoAtNet-rmlp-2 (claimed public LB 0.949 alone; the 224 centre-crop version 0.945) plus a ConvNeXt 2.5D reader. Their blend layers add only LB-tuned noise.

That CoAtNet went from 0.924 to 0.949 mainly through data:
- OAI external data, credited in their notebook;
- multi-source weak labels;
- a larger corpus (+0.013 on gold);
- 96 slices / 94 windows, native 384 px, SWA, anatomical mirror TTA.

Plan:
1. **Exp 1:** their recipe on our data, to split the gap into architecture vs data.
2. **Exp 2:** + weak localisation from report text, 3D DICOM coordinates, severity heads.
3. Then the label loop (strictly out-of-fold) and external data, ordered by the Exp 1 result.

| Exp | What | Pre-registered acceptance | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| **1** | **Public CoAtNet recipe on our fold 0.** Backbone `coatnet_rmlp_2_rw_384.sw_in12k_ft_in1k` with their per-finding attention head (RaptorClassifier). Data: their d96 corpus (5 slots, 96 slices over 2–98%, 140 mm at 384 px, per-series 2/98 window), rebuilt on our studies by Kaggle CPU kernels `tumis001/rsna-coatnet-d96-cache-shard-{0..3}`. Train at 384 with 16 stratified windows; evaluate on the centre 320 crop with 94 windows. Our choices (their training script is not public): AdamW 1e-4 / 1e-3 head, wd 0.05, 1 warm-up epoch + cosine, 15 epochs, effective batch 8, bf16; augmentation = anatomical mirror, ±10° rotation, ±10% scale, ±6% shift, gamma/gain. HPO-v3.1-D2 labels, gold excluded. One Modal H100 (no multi-GPU: the earlier two-T4 DataParallel r50 lost 0.0085). Code: `scripts/coatnet_d96_runner.py`, `scripts/modal_coatnet_d96.py`, scorer `scripts/audit_coatnet_d96.py` | **Primary:** weight average of epochs 11–15 (fixed in advance, BatchNorm refreshed), 320 crop, no TTA, vs d2_224 0.8963 on Steven-v4 fold 0. **PASS** if Δ ≥ +0.005 with ≥ 90% of 2,000 paired bootstraps better. Secondary only: mirror TTA, best epoch, gold-58, quad + new model. If it passes, submit for an LB anchor | **FAIL 2026-10-10.** SWA 0.8982, Δ +0.0018 [−0.0039, +0.0075], 71.5% of bootstraps better. Quad + SWA (equal mean) +0.0018 [+0.0008, +0.0030], 100% of bootstraps better: diverse but not stronger. Gold-58 0.905 (development only). D2 rule states explain why (2026-10-12, train folds vs ruler, gold-58 counts): the 0.3–0.7 band mixes **BORD** cells ("mild"; gold mostly positive: effusion 18+/2−, MCL 5/1, medial OA 6/0) and **BELOW** cells (trace / below threshold; ruler positive, gold mostly negative: effusion 9+/13−, MCL 1/6, ACL 0/4). 3a pushed both up; the BELOW half is the likely harm. Reading: (1) fold 0 rewards ranking mild findings as positive, the LB does not, which explains why fold 0 misjudges label changes (1e, 3a, 2d) but not recipe changes; (2) our soft labels (mild ≈ 0.44–0.61) are closer to the competition convention than the ruler; 3a moved toward the ruler and lost on the LB; (3) within the mild band the report text carries little information about the competition label (≈ 0.5), so only the image can separate those cases. Caveats: 58 studies, gold selection unknown, severity from one LLM reader |
| 2-band | **Which fold-0 band tracks the LB? (CPU, retrospective; prediction stated before running: LB follows the clear band).** For the 7 submitted d96 singles (1b, 1c, 1e, 1e-s2, 3a, 3b, 2d), fold-0 AUC on all confident cells vs on mild-band positives (report severity not moderate+, incl. hedged / reader-absent) vs on clear-band positives (moderate+), each against all confident negatives; correlation with public LB | Diagnostic | **Done 2026-10-12: prediction held.** Pearson / Spearman vs LB: **all-cells 12-target −0.16 / −0.20, 10-target −0.11 / −0.07; mild band −0.24 / −0.42; clear band 12-target +0.90 / +0.73, 10-target +0.81 / +0.55.** The two LB losers have the lowest clear-band AUC (12-target: 3a 0.9705, 2d 0.9638; others 0.9735–0.9778) while their all-cells scores looked normal (0.9064, 0.9032). Across these runs the headline fold-0 macro carries no LB signal; the clear band does. Caveats: 7 points, LB noise ±0.002, clear-band positives are few per target (wide per-run CIs), runs differ in labels / heads / backbone. **Use: report the clear-band 12-target AUC for every candidate and require it not to drop; treat mild-band movement as convention-dependent.** **Exp 4b on this readout: clear band 12-target 0.9710** (1e 0.9758 / 0.9750; 3a 0.9705, 2d 0.9638), 10-target 0.9784 (1e 0.9794 / 0.9817): its fold-0 gain is all in the mild band and its clear band is at the level of the LB losers, a warning against submitting or building on it without an LB check. **Refinement:** several targets have very few clear positives (contusion 2, synovitis 3, MCL 5, fracture 8, Baker's 11). On the six targets with ≥ 20 (ACL, medial meniscus, medial OA, lateral OA, PF OA, effusion) the clear band still tracks the LB (Pearson +0.94, Spearman +0.73; LB losers 3a 0.9649, 2d 0.9658; others 0.9690–0.9719) and **4b is the lowest of all: 0.9617** (lateral OA 0.8805 vs 1e 0.937 / 0.918, n 21; PF OA 0.9537 vs 0.961 / 0.964, n 102) |
| 4b-abl | **Ablation of Exp 4b (inference only).** Kaggle T4 kernel `tumis001/rsna-d96-r50-native-ablation`: native builder on the 825 fold-0 studies, 4b SWA, centre 320, one window per slice; attention head re-applied with slots masked; thinning = coronal / axial attention limited to 18 evenly spaced windows. Parity with the runner: macro12 0.9068 = 0.9068, max \|dp\| 0.012. Compared with 1e's ablation (2-abl). Scorer `scripts/audit_d96_native_ablation.py` | Diagnostic | **Done 2026-10-12. 4b spreads its reliance across planes.** Macro change on removal (12-target), 4b vs 1e: sagittal fluid −0.024 vs −0.040; whole sagittal −0.036 vs −0.077; coronal fluid −0.015 vs −0.010; **axial −0.006 vs −0.000 (now used: effusion −0.017, synovitis −0.013, MCL −0.013, contusion −0.013)**; only-sagittal −0.034 vs −0.012. Coronal fluid carries more: MCL −0.067 vs −0.043, fracture −0.037 vs −0.015. **The losses sit in the sagittal-read targets whose sagittal reliance fell:** PF OA drop-sagittal −0.068 vs −0.211, lateral OA −0.046 vs −0.116, Baker's −0.081 vs −0.173; lateral OA full 0.852 vs 0.864, PF OA 0.884 vs 0.889; no slot mask recovers them at inference (the features changed, not only the attention). Same trade as 2c (more non-sagittal voice: MCL / effusion / Baker's up, OA down) and both fail the clear-band screen. Coronal non-fluid was not the cause (1e drop: ≤ 0.001 on every target except effusion −0.004). **Density is not needed at inference:** thin coronal / axial / both −0.001 (macro), MCL −0.004: the gain comes from training on the denser / fluid-axial input, so a submission can evaluate ~94 windows. `data/native_ablation/` |
| 2-route | **Per-target plane routing, zero-training proxy (CPU).** Before a GPU run of anatomy-fixed routing (sagittal-read OA / PF OA / Baker's keep 1e's pool; coronal-read MCL / fracture / contusion get more coronal / axial voice), stitch saved fold-0 predictions by anatomy, variants fixed before scoring: S-4b (MCL, fracture, contusion from 4b, rest 1e), S-2c (same from 2c), S-4b+ (OA + Baker's from 1e, all others mean(1e, 4b)); controls mean(1e, 4b) and mean(1e, 1e-s2) (averaging with no plane change). Scorer `scripts/audit_d96_routing_stitch.py` | Gate stated before scoring: a routing GPU run only if a stitch beats 1e on fold 0 and keeps the clear band. The averaging-control comparison was added in the scorer design (before scoring) but its +0.003 bar was set after | **Done 2026-10-12: routing not worth a GPU run.** 10-target vs 1e 0.9073: **S-4b +0.0021 [−0.0009, +0.0054], 91%; S-2c +0.0017, 78%** (only MCL +0.012 / +0.024, fracture +0.006 / −0.006 move); S-4b+ +0.0052, 100%, but **mean(1e, 1e-s2) gives +0.0044 with no plane change**, so S-4b+ is mostly averaging (routing-specific ≈ +0.001); mean(1e, 4b) +0.0060 but clear6 −0.0018 (lateral OA 0.918). Clear-band (6 targets ≥ 20) cannot judge routing: the coronal-read targets have 2–8 clear positives, and the stitches keep 1e's OA (S-4b+ 0.9730 vs 1e 0.9719). Ceiling of routing on fold 0 ≈ +0.002, below the +0.003 bar and a seed spread; MCL's +0.012 is the only real per-target signal |
| 5a | **In-domain self-supervised start (Barlow Twins) + 1e recipe.** Motivation: misses are mild findings the model looks at but does not recognise (2-side, 2-sev); recipe changes that redistribute attention only trade targets (2c, 2d, 4b, 2-route); features come from 2017 supervised ImageNet (`tv_in1k`). Step 1 (`scripts/ssl_barlow_d96.py`, Modal H100): start from the **Barlow Twins ImageNet r50** (facebookresearch/barlowtwins, MIT; chosen from public benchmarks without our own checkpoint runs: Hosseinzadeh Taher et al. 2021, arXiv 2108.05930, name SwAV / Barlow Twins / SeLa-v2 / DeepCluster-v2 as consistently beating supervised ImageNet on 7 medical tasks; the other three are CC-BY-NC); continue Barlow Twins (projector 8192³, λ 0.0051, new projector trained alone for 500 steps, then AdamW backbone 1e-4 / projector 1e-3, cosine) for **10 window-epochs** on 3-slice d96 windows of the **3,524 training-fold studies only (no fold 0, no gold; no labels)**; two GPU views: resized crop 224 (area 0.25–1), anatomical mirror, ±15°, gamma / gain, blur, noise. Step 2: runner `D96_PHASE=r50_ssl_pertarget` = Exp 1e exactly (d96, per-target labels sha d40803d0…, seed 2026, schedule, SWA 21–25), backbone from the final SSL epoch; outputs `run_r50_ssl_pertarget/`. Step 0: timing bench before step 1 | **Pre-registered 2026-10-12.** Primary: SWA 21–25, no TTA. **PASS if both:** (a) 10-target Δ vs the mean of 1e's two seeds (0.9073 / 0.9097) ≥ +0.003 with ≥ 90% of 2,000 paired bootstraps better against each seed; (b) clear-band 6-target (≥ 20 clear positives, 2-band) ≥ 0.9690 (the lower 1e seed). PASS → submit standalone; LB read vs 1e two-seed 0.935. **Early stop (saves the run):** kill if 10-target at epoch 12 is below both 1e seeds' epoch 12 (0.8912 / 0.8919) by > 0.010. Secondary: per target, mild vs clear band, gold-58 (noise only), SSL loss / feature-spread curve, ensemble value. A FAIL does not separate "Barlow Twins ImageNet start" from "in-domain continuation" (one run by request; no checkpoint-selection runs) | **FAIL 2026-10-12 on both conditions.** SSL: 10 epochs × 210 s on one H100; view agreement (1 − √(on/8192)) 0.66 → 0.84 at epoch 6, then drifted back to 0.83 (on 211 → 230) by epoch 10; feature spread steady (no collapse). Fine-tune: SWA 21–25 **10-target 0.8997 vs 1e 0.9073 / 0.9097, Δ −0.0077 [−0.0131, −0.0020] / −0.0100 [−0.0155, −0.0047], 0–0.4% better**; 12-target 0.8964; mirror −0.0075. **Clear band 6-target 0.9596** (gate ≥ 0.9690; lowest of any d96 run, below 4b 0.9617). Curve: **fast start, lower plateau**: ahead of both 1e seeds by +0.029 at epochs 2–3, +0.004 at 10–13, behind from 14 and −0.007 by 19; flat 0.897 from epoch 20 while 1e kept rising. Per target vs seed mean: MCL −0.025, PF OA −0.023, ACL −0.011, lateral meniscus −0.011; medial OA +0.005, effusion +0.002. Gold-58 0.898 (MCL 0.810 vs 0.90). Ensemble: L4 1e → 5a −0.0004, L4 + 5a −0.0001: no value. Not submitted. **Frozen linear probe** (Kaggle T4 kernel `tumis001/rsna-d96-ssl-probe` v2 on cache shard 0 only, 878 train / 203 fold-0 studies, so ±0.02 noise; 32 windows, 320 crop, mean-pooled; L2 logistic on 256 PCs; scorer `scripts/audit_ssl_probe.py`, `data/ssl_probe/audit_ssl_probe.txt`), 10-target all-mean / plane-means: tv_in1k 0.735 / 0.759, Barlow Twins ImageNet 0.758 / 0.761, **SSL epoch 2 0.791 / 0.803**, epoch 5 0.782 / 0.795, epoch 10 0.778 / 0.787. **In-domain SSL improved frozen features (+0.03–0.04 over both ImageNet starts), best at epoch 2 and declining after**, matching the fast fine-tune start; but the advantage did not survive 25 epochs of full fine-tuning, which ended lower. Unresolved cause (untested): invariances learned from intensity / blur / noise augmentation limiting what fine-tuning can recover (MCL, PF OA hit hardest), or fine-tune settings tuned for supervised weights. `run_r50_ssl_pertarget/`, `audit_r50_ssl_pertarget.txt` |
| 5b-probe | **OrthoDiffusion go/no-go (frozen probe, inference only).** Motivation: first-principles review after 5a: pretraining can only add information if it sees knee MRI we do not have; OAI is excluded. **OrthoDiffusion** (Lan et al., arXiv 2602.20752, Feb 2026, MIT; weights `lanstat0123/orthodiffusion`, code `lt-0123/OrthoDiffusion`): three 3D U-Net DDPM denoisers (sagittal / coronal / axial, 34.5M params each, 21,499 steps × batch 10) pretrained on **15,948 unlabelled knee MRIs from three Chinese hospital centres** (not OAI / fastMRI / MRNet); paper input: PD series, 16 central slices, 256², min-max [−1, 1]; paper result 8-finding macro AUROC 90.8 vs 3D ResNet-18 from scratch 83.2 (no ImageNet-2D baseline). Safety: pickles disassembled (only OrderedDict / tensor-rebuild globals), loaded `weights_only=True` offline, EMA state dicts strict-loaded, no non-finite values; re-saved as plain tensors (private dataset `tumis001/rsna-orthodiffusion-weights`). Features (`scripts/od_probe_features.py` via `scripts/modal_od_probe.py`, one Modal H100, bf16, **all cache shards, 4,349 non-gold studies**): one series per plane (sagittal slot 0 else 1, coronal 2 else 3, axial 4), 16 central valid rows, 384 → 256; 2 in-plane orientations × t ∈ {50, 150, 250}; mid_0 / mid_2 → global mean (256) or 2×2 grid (1,024) per plane, planes concatenated; denoising error at t = 150 per orientation (label-free domain / orientation check). ImageNet r50 `tv_in1k` reference from the same run (Exp 5a probe recipe). Probe `scripts/audit_od_probe.py`: as 5a's (PCA-256, L2 logistic, λ on inner fold 1, 1e labels, fold-0 Steven-v4 ruler); **the OD setting is chosen on inner fold 1 only** (24 settings), never on fold 0. A first Kaggle T4 kernel (`tumis001/rsna-d96-orthodiffusion-probe`, shard 0, fp32) was superseded by the Modal run (~25 min, ~$2) | **Pre-registered 2026-10-12, before any probe result.** Continue to a fine-tune (5b: fused r50 + OD encoders, 1e recipe) only if **(a)** selected OD setting − tv_in1k ≥ +0.09 on the fold-0 10-target (the shard-0 bar 0.85 vs 0.759, made relative because all shards raise every probe score), **or (b)** tv_in1k + selected OD − tv_in1k ≥ +0.03 (complementary information). Otherwise close OrthoDiffusion. Secondary: clear-band 6, per target, denoising error by orientation / plane. Caveat: 5a's +0.03–0.04 frozen advantage did not survive fine-tuning, hence the large margins | **FAIL 2026-10-12 on both conditions; OrthoDiffusion closed.** 3,524 train / 825 fold-0 studies. **tv_in1k reference 10-target 0.7835, clear6 0.8747.** Selected OD (inner fold 1: transposed, t 50, mid_0, 2×2 grid) **0.7544 (−0.029; gate +0.09), clear6 0.8548**; best OD setting on fold 0 0.7553, so selection did not matter. **tv_in1k + OD 0.7840 (+0.0005; gate +0.03)**, clear6 0.8713 (−0.003); inner fold 1 +0.013 did not carry to fold 0. Per target OD alone trails r50 on every target except contusion / fracture (small, noisy); MCL 0.636 vs 0.637, ACL 0.714 vs 0.742, PF OA 0.746 vs 0.795. Settings barely matter (all 24 within 0.727–0.755); **transposed orientation beats as-cached in every setting (+0.01–0.02)**, as the label-free denoising error predicted (t 150: sagittal 0.090 vs 0.107, coronal 0.082 vs 0.091, axial 0.090 either). Reading: OD's frozen features carry less finding information than ImageNet r50 on our data and nothing r50 lacks; with a linear head, 16 central slices × one series per plane and a 16×16 bottleneck do not compete with 32 whole-knee windows. Fine-tuning (5b) not run. `data/orthodiffusion_probe/audit_od_probe.txt`, features `data/orthodiffusion_probe/modal_output/od_probe/` |
| 2-evid | **Missing-evidence audit (CPU, correlational).** Hypothesis: training sees 16 stratified windows over all 96 rows (≈ 3 from coronal fluid) vs 94 at evaluation, so positive bags of small / mild findings often contain no window showing the finding (MIL label noise on exactly the mild band). Test on 1e's saved per-window positive CAM mass (attention-side kernel, 825 fold-0 studies): for each detected positive (rank ≥ median of negatives), windows holding 50% of the evidence (n50), top-window share, P(the runner's 16-window draw contains a top-3 evidence window), mass in draw; mild vs moderate+. Scorer `scripts/audit_d96_window_evidence.py` | Diagnostic; hypothesis predicts mild positives more concentrated (lower n50, lower capture) than moderate+ | **Done 2026-10-12: hypothesis not supported.** 2,425 detected positives: evidence is spread, n50 median 4–10 windows (Baker's 4, menisci / contusion 5, ACL / PF OA / fracture 6, MCL 9, OA / effusion 10), top window 8–20% of mass; a 16-window draw holds 0.17 of the mass (uniform) and a top-3 window 46–48% of the time for every target. **Mild and moderate+ do not differ** (n50 8 vs 6; capture 0.470 vs 0.473; per target within ±0.04). Caveats: positive mass only (signed evidence was not saved), the model's own view of evidence, detected positives only. Reading: window sub-sampling is not why mild findings are missed. Side fact: ≈ 5.7 of the 16 training windows (36%) land in sagittal / coronal non-fluid series, which 2-abl measured as contributing ≤ 0.003. `data/attention_side_audit/audit_window_evidence.txt` |
| 6a | **SWA with a cyclical tail (within-run ensemble).** Motivation (evidence): the single model is variance-limited: mean(1e, 1e-s2) +0.0044 fold-0 10-target, L4 − 1e +0.007 LB; 2c / 5a led 1c mid-run by up to +0.010 and fell back to the same plateau; current SWA averages epochs 21–25 at the end of a cosine (LR 0.04–0.01 of peak), so its members are near-identical (within-run soups +0.0004–0.0009), while cross-run soups collapse (different basins); 1b's restart dipped and then gained +0.0086. Change: runner `D96_PHASE=r50_swa_cyclic` = Exp 1e exactly (labels sha d40803d0…, seed 2026, schedule) for epochs 1–17; epochs 18–25 = 8 one-epoch cosine cycles, each restarting at the level the cosine had reached at the start of epoch 18 (0.2575 of peak, continuous) down to the 1% floor; SWA = mean of the 8 cycle ends (epochs 18–25), BN refreshed as before. Same compute as 1e. Outputs `run_r50_swa_cyclic/` | **Pre-registered 2026-10-12, before launch.** Primary: SWA 18–25, 320 crop, no TTA. **PASS if both:** (a) 10-target Δ vs the mean of 1e's two seeds (0.9073 / 0.9097) ≥ +0.003 with ≥ 90% of 2,000 paired bootstraps better against each seed; (b) clear-band 6-target ≥ 0.9690. Secondary: per-cycle-end val AUC, prediction-level average of the 8 cycle ends vs the weight average, rank correlation between cycle ends (diversity) vs 1e's epochs 21–25, mirror TTA, per target, ensemble value | **FAIL (2026-10-12).** SWA 18–25 10-target 0.9059 vs 1e 0.9073 / 0.9097: Δ −0.0015 [−0.0049, +0.0022] (19.8% better) and −0.0038 [−0.0077, +0.0001] (2.6%); mean Δ −0.0026. 12-target 0.9031 vs 0.9037 / 0.9053. Clear-band 6 0.9652 < 0.9690 (1e 0.9719 / 0.9690). Mirror TTA −0.0012 vs 1e mirror. Rank corr vs 1e s2026 0.958. Cycle-end val 12-target 0.9018 / 0.9029 / 0.8997 / 0.9025 / 0.9014 / 0.9018 / 0.8998 / 0.9021: cycling buys no member above 1e's plateau. Per target it trades ACL / Contusion / PF OA (−0.007 to −0.009) for Baker's / Fracture (+0.008 to +0.010). Swapping 1e → 6a in L4: −0.0009 (2.3% better); L4 + 6a: −0.0010. Gold-58 0.908 (1e 0.905 / 0.912). Epochs 1–17 track 1e within seed noise. Higher-LR cycles cost the clear-band targets and add no usable diversity. Closed; not submitted. |
| 6b | **Series-allocated training windows.** Motivation (evidence): 2-abl: removing sagittal / coronal non-fluid moves macro AUC by ≤ 0.003 (every target), yet ≈ 5.7 of the 16 stratified training windows land there and only ≈ 3 in coronal fluid, the series MCL / fracture / contusion depend on (dropping it: MCL −0.043); every change giving coronal more weight raised MCL (4b +0.012, 2c +0.023). Per target vs seed mean: **MCL +0.014** (as predicted; gold 0.937 vs 0.90), Synovitis +0.003, but Lateral OA −0.012, Contusion −0.010, Medial Meniscus −0.009, PF OA −0.007, Effusion −0.006, ACL −0.005. Reading rules: mean ≤ head 1 → B adds nothing in one backbone; head 1 ≤ 1e → D (or sharing) does not help; tops already in a random bag ≥ ~80% → D changed little. Preflight (CPU, in the chain): both label hashes and the same 4,349 studies; avg == mean of head probabilities; train → 24 logits; attention sums to 1; a guided bag starts with the exact top-window slices; gradients reach both classifiers and the backbone; guide refresh end to end and stable on re-run. Per target (SWA vs 1e) losses concentrate on fracture, Baker's, MCL, medial meniscus. **Reading:** self-selected training windows hurt this MIL model: training bags rich in its own top windows differ from the uniform 94-window evaluation pool, and the selection reinforces early (near-uniform) attention; random stratified coverage is doing useful work. Training windows = contiguous runs at stride 3 within each slot, [4, 4, 3, 2, 3] = 16 (1e's slot shares), random start; slots too short pass windows to slots with room, then 1e stratified fill. | B2 | **Model B v2: Swin3D-T regularised (frozen early stages, one shared stage 4, token attention, MR augmentation) on label-v2.** Runner `scripts/swin3d_d96n_runner.py` with `SWIN_PHASE=swin3d_b2` (B1 path unchanged; CPU smoke test of both paths passed). Motivation: B1's val12 peaked at epoch 10 (0.889) and val BCE rose from 0.492 to 0.532 by SWA (overfit), and B1 had no localisation (stage-4 tokens averaged over space). Changes vs B1: **labels label-v2** (teammate, sha dd0ab9ab…, LB 0.937 on the 1e recipe; copied to the Modal assets); **patch embedding + stages 1–2 frozen** (no gradient, no drop path); **one stage-4 branch shared by all planes** (trainable 26.5M of 28.1M vs 59.0M); **drop path 0.25** (torchvision's linear rule over the 12 blocks; B1 0.1); **layer-wise lr decay 0.9 per unit** from B1's 5e-5 at the final norm down to 1.9e-5 at the first stage-3 block (no layer faster than B1, in the direction of B1's pre-registered tuning rule); head 1e-3, wd 0.05 as B1; **per-finding attention over every stage-4 token** (T/2 × 10 × 10 per series, series embedding added) instead of one spatial mean per depth; **15 epochs, cosine to 1%, SWA 11–15**; val scored at epochs 1, 3, 5, 7, 9–15. Training augmentation: mirror p 0.5 per study (as B1); affine / gamma / gain at 1e's ranges drawn **per series**; **series dropout** p 0.1 each (one always kept); **every second slice** p 0.25 (series ≥ 16 rows); smooth multiplicative **bias field** (4×4 bicubic, amplitude ≤ exp(±0.3)) p 0.3; Gaussian **blur** or **unsharp mask** (σ 0.5–1.0 px) p 0.2 each; **Gaussian noise** (σ ≤ 0.03) p 0.2. No depth crop (series ends hold patella, suprapatellar recess, popliteal fossa, compartment edges: B1's weakest targets), no mixup / cutout / elastic. Eval as B1 (centre 320, all series, no TTA). Profile first (`--swin-profile-run --phase swin3d_b2`: preflight + drop path / frozen / optimizer-group / augmentation checks + timing at 320), then the run spec and cost go to the user. Deviation from B1's tuning rule (B2 = lr 2.5e-5): the rule addressed only the late slide (~0.005), not the 0.018 clear6 gap at B1's peak; this B2 design was chosen with the user (2026-10-14). B2 is run 2 of the ≤ 3-run budget. **Pre-launch review (agent, 2026-10-14), applied before any launch:** profile now uses the fused attention the run uses (it timed torchvision's, ~30% slower); the launcher's stale-runner stop also matches the Swin runner (a preemption retry would otherwise fail on volume reload); the EPOCH line is printed after weights / recovery are saved; recovery also stores torch CPU / CUDA RNG states; blur / noise run in fp32 under autocast; depth crop removed, series dropout 0.2 → 0.1, lr decay 0.85 → 0.9 (underfit risk from stacked regularisation) | **Pre-registered 2026-10-14, before any launch.** Primary as B1 (SWA, 320, no TTA; `scripts/audit_phase1.py`); gates unchanged from B1: **floor** 10-target ≥ 0.900; **diversity** mean(B2, 1e) 10-target ≥ 0.9147; **single-model LB candidate** clear6 ≥ 0.9745 AND 10-target ≥ 0.9053. **Submit to LB if** the floor passes AND clear6 ≥ 0.9680 (clear-band fit → LB ≥ ~0.930, the level where a pair with 1e / label-v2 could pay); otherwise no submission. **Early stop (runner, `kill`):** val macro (12 targets, confident cells) < 0.880 at epoch 9 → run stops, no SWA (B1: 0.882 at ep 5, 0.889 at ep 10 of a slower 25-epoch schedule). **Underfit reading (diagnostic):** final train loss ≥ 0.40 (1e ≈ 0.38, B1 0.299) with val BCE still falling at epoch 15 → B2 under-trained, not over-regularised-and-weak. **Overfit check (diagnostic, not a gate):** val BCE at SWA within 0.010 of its minimum and the minimum at epoch ≥ 9 (B1: min 0.492 at ep 10, SWA 0.532). Confound stated now: B2 changes labels and model together, so a gain cannot be split between them (label-v2 was +0.001 LB on the 1e recipe, so most of any gain would be the model). Lateral OA (every training label changed) and MCL (173 cells) are still scored against the unchanged fold-0 reference, so per-target deltas on those two read the label convention as well as the model. Secondary: per target vs 1e / B1 (B1's worst: Lateral OA, PF OA, Effusion, Medial OA), clear / mild band, rank correlation with 1e, mirror TTA, best epoch, gold-58 (dev only). If B2 also stays below the floor, Model B closes (B3 not run) | **Profile done 2026-10-14** (`experiments/coatnet_d96_fold0/swin3d_b2_profile/profile.log` on the volume, ~$1): PREFLIGHT_OK (weights sha 7615ae03…, 4,407 manifests, crop = identity warp 8e-6, mirror rule); 28.1M params, 26.5M trainable; drop path 0 → 0.25; 5 frozen modules, 20 optimizer groups, backbone lr 1.94e-5…5e-5, head 1e-3; AUG2_OK on 200 training studies (mean series kept 3.56, all kept 65%, rows kept 0.79); tokens T/2 × 100 per series. **320 fused: train 0.083 s/study, eval 0.060, peak 7.0 GiB → 5.7 min/epoch, ~1.43 h for 15 epochs** (B1 0.163 s/study, 4.1 h). **Launched 2026-10-14 21:51 CEST with the user's go** (Modal app ap-cFOJOS5gQcYxsgimRZ8EsU, `train_b` H100 / 8 CPU / 32 GiB, out `run_swin3d_b2`), estimate ~1.5 h / ~$7–8 (~$4 if the epoch-9 stop triggers) **B2 result 2026-10-14: FAIL on all three gates; overfit fixed** (`data/coatnet_d96_fold0/audit_phase1_swin3d_b2.txt`, `run_swin3d_b2/`, SWA weights sha 7853700f…). | B3-abl | **Model B3 ablations on Kaggle (free T4 x2): which change closes B2's fine-structure gap.** Motivation (user request 2026-10-15, after B2 FAIL): vs 1e-label-v2 on the same labels B2 loses ACL −0.055, Lateral Meniscus −0.038, PF OA −0.041, Lateral OA −0.029 (all cells) and is level / ahead on Effusion, Synovitis, Baker's, Contusion, Fracture. Diagnostics (CPU, `scripts/diag_swin3d_b3.py`; output `diagnostics/b3_diag.txt` in Kaggle `tumis001/rsna-swin3d-model-b-review`): B1 (nothing frozen) at its epoch 10 had ACL 0.898 / clear-band 0.982 and PF OA clear-band 0.935 vs B2 0.855 / 0.977 / 0.910, so freezing is the first suspect (confounded in B1→B2 with 5 other changes); B2's gap does not depend on slice thickness (thick − thin gap: ACL −0.005 [−0.061, +0.053], Lateral Meniscus +0.014 [−0.056, +0.076]) but B1-ep10's ACL gap does (−0.002 thin vs −0.041 thick); forum (sweep 3, 2026-10-15): dense depth sampling carries the 2.5D singles (737696, 746792, 743148), frozen backbones miss ligaments (738036 Pillar-0 0.697 → 0.797 fine-tuned, 747553), no forum report of temporal patch size or of a 3D model beating 2.5D. Pair facts: mean(B2, 1e-label-v2) clear6 0.9713 / 10-target 0.9117 vs mean(P1-cnx, 1e-label-v2) 0.9763 / 0.9164; rank correlation with 1e-label-v2: B2 0.845, P1-cnx 0.854. **Arms** (B2 recipe exactly, label-v2, same augmentation; only the named change): **A0** B2 (patch embedding + stages 1–2 frozen), control on the Kaggle setup; **A1** nothing frozen (layer-wise lr decay 0.9 continued down to the patch embedding: 9.3e-6 … 5e-5; drop path rule unchanged); **A2** A1 + temporal patch 1 (patch embedding kernel 1×4×4 = the Kinetics 2×4×4 kernel summed over its 2 frames, exact on a repeated slice; checked to 2e-6; 2× tokens). Setup: `kaggle/experiment_kernels/swin3d_ablation/run.py` (kernel `tumis001/rsna-swin3d-b3-ablation`, code + assets dataset `tumis001/rsna-swin3d-ablation-code`, cache = the six d96n shard kernel outputs), runner + `SWIN_AMP=fp16` (GradScaler; T4 has no bf16) + `SWIN_CFG` overrides (unset = Modal B1 / B2 unchanged; CPU smoke of B1, B2 and an override passed), workers 2 per arm, one arm per GPU. **Schedule:** short and identical for all arms, warm-up 1 epoch, cosine to 1%, SWA of the last 2 epochs; length E = the largest of {8, 6} that fits every arm in ≤ 11 h on one T4 by the Kaggle profile (run first, free), fixed before any training. A2 runs only if it fits at E (gradient checkpointing allowed) | **Pre-registered 2026-10-15, before the profile and any training.** Fold 0, SWA of the last 2 epochs, 320, no TTA; scored with the audit's clear band / all-cell AUC. **Fine-4** = mean all-cell AUC of ACL, Lateral Meniscus, Medial Meniscus, PF OA. **Unfreeze adopted for B3** if A1 − A0 ≥ +0.010 on fine-4 AND val12 A1 ≥ A0 − 0.002. **Temporal patch 1 adopted** if A2 − A1 ≥ +0.008 on fine-4 AND val12 A2 ≥ A1 − 0.002. **B3 go / no-go (recommendation to the user, not automatic):** projected B3 clear6 = B2's 0.9542 + (adopted arm − A0 clear6) + 0.005 (full-length schedule, from the B2 LR-tail estimate); **single-model case** only if projected ≥ 0.9745; **partner case** only if projected ≥ 0.965 (pair has to beat mean(P1-cnx, 1e-label-v2) clear6 0.9763, which no partner within 0.01 of 1e-label-v2 has done without label diversity); below 0.965 → Model B closes. Caveats: one seed per arm, between-run per-target CI ±0.004–0.019 (fine-4 mean roughly ±0.008), short schedules read early learning and cannot show B1-style late overfit; fp16 vs bf16 differs from the Modal runs (A0 is the control). Secondary: per target, clear band, val BCE curve, train loss, s/study | **Profile (Kaggle T4, fp16, 2026-10-15, kernel v1 / v2, `data/kaggle_kernel_outputs/swin3d_b3_ablation_profile_v{1,2}/`):** weights sha 7615ae03… (torchvision), PREFLIGHT_OK and AUG2_OK on all arms. Train / eval s per study, peak GiB: A0 0.544 / 0.40, 6.3 (37 min / epoch); A1 without checkpointing **OOM** (16 GB), A1 + checkpointing 1.281 / 0.43, 4.3 (81 min / epoch); A2 without checkpointing OOM, A2 + checkpointing 2.29 / 0.78, 6.8 (145 min / epoch). **E = 6** (8 epochs ≈ 10.9 h for A1, at the 11 h limit; 6 ≈ 8.3 h): warm-up 1, cosine to 1%, SWA 5–6, val scored at epochs 2 / 4 / 5 / 6. **A2 does not fit** (6 epochs ≈ 15 h) → not run on Kaggle, as pre-registered (on a Modal H100 it would be a paid run, user's call). Checkpointing changes memory and time only (RNG state preserved). Training kernel v3: A0 on GPU 0, A1 + checkpointing on GPU 1, launched 2026-10-15 **Result 2026-10-15 (kernel v3 COMPLETE 16:06; A0 274 min, A1 509 min; `data/kaggle_kernel_outputs/swin3d_b3_ablation_train_v3/`, scored by `scripts/score_swin3d_b3_ablation.py`): UNFREEZE NOT ADOPTED → B3 not run, Model B closes as pre-registered.** SWA 5–6, no TTA: A0 fine-4 0.8532 / val12 0.8657 / clear6 0.9298 / 10-target 0.8675; A1 0.8464 / 0.8624 / 0.9261 / 0.8631. **A1 − A0: fine-4 −0.0069 [−0.0113, −0.0023] (rule ≥ +0.010; 0% of bootstrap > 0), val12 −0.0032 (rule ≥ −0.002), clear6 −0.0036, 10-target −0.0044**: the wrong direction on all four fine targets (ACL −0.006, Lateral Meniscus −0.017, Medial Meniscus −0.005, PF OA 0.000; MCL −0.011; Baker's +0.006, Synovitis / Contusion +0.002). Projected B3 clear6 = 0.9542 − 0.0036 + 0.005 = 0.9556 < 0.965 (partner) → Model B closes. Curves: val12 still rising at epoch 6 in both arms (A0 0.830 → 0.860 → 0.865 → 0.866), A1 behind at every scored epoch; val BCE A0 0.522 / A1 0.524 at epoch 6; train loss equal (0.433 / 0.435). Mirror TTA does not change the ordering (A1 − A0 fine-4 −0.0059). A0 on the 6-epoch fp16 Kaggle schedule sits well below B2's 15-epoch Modal result (fine-4 0.853 vs 0.892), as expected from the short schedule. Reading: in this short schedule the early-stage Kinetics filters are better left frozen; B1's epoch-10 ACL / PF OA advantage came from something other than unfreezing (per-plane stage-4 branches, flat LR, or label set). Caveats: one seed per arm; 6 epochs read early learning only, so a longer unfrozen run could still catch up, but the pre-registered rule does not allow B3 on that. A2 (temporal patch 1) not run (conditional on A1). SWA weights kept in the kernel output (A0 / A1 `weights_swa5_6.pt`); cost $0 (Kaggle) |
| E-ref | **Ensemble refresh on the LB: does a ConvNeXt or a Swin3D member add to L4 (0.943)?** User request 2026-10-15 ("do 2": ensemble refresh) after Model B closed. Members are existing fold-0 weights only (no training, no all-data retrain). **E1 = L4 + P1-cnx** (5 members, equal probability mean): kernel = `rsna-lean4-d96-probability-ensemble` with the P1-cnx ConvNeXt-T (`tumis001/rsna-d96-cnxt-p1-pertarget-fold0`, weights sha 0125e766…) added to the d96 member list (same windows / crop / normalisation as in the 1e + P1-cnx pair kernel, LB 0.938). **E2 = L4 + B2** (5 members): L4 kernel plus the B2 Swin3D submission script (weights sha 7853700f…) run as a subprocess on the same test studies, B2 probabilities averaged in as the fifth member (studies B2 cannot read fall back to the 4-member mean). Fold-0 screen (CPU, `scripts/screen_ensemble_refresh.py`, 1,000 paired bootstraps vs L4 clear6 0.9742 / 10-target 0.9201): **L4+cnx clear6 +0.0015 [+0.0003, +0.0030], 10-target +0.0014 [+0.0003, +0.0024]**; L4+B2 −0.0005 [−0.0020, +0.0009] / +0.0005 [−0.0005, +0.0016]; L4 1e→label-v2 −0.0002 / −0.0000; L4 + label-v2 +0.0009 / −0.0004; L4 1e→label-v2 + cnx +0.0015 / +0.0014; 6-member (1e→label-v2, + cnx, + B2) +0.0011 / +0.0017 | **Pre-registered 2026-10-15, before any E-ref submission.** Each arm: one submission, judged on public LB vs L4 0.943 (LB SD ≈ 0.003; the unrounded rank inside a tie block is reported as a secondary signal). **E1:** ≥ 0.944 → E1 becomes the ensemble base; 0.942–0.943 → tie, L4 kept (lean: fewer members); ≤ 0.941 → rejected. **E2:** fold 0 underrates Swin3D (B1 / B2 LB residuals +0.006 / +0.013), so the LB decides: ≥ 0.944 → B2 earns a place; ≤ 0.943 → B2 dropped from all ensembles (Model B fully closed). Order: E1, then E2. If both reach ≥ 0.944, a 6-member L4 + cnx + B2 is tried only if E1 and E2 together suggest it adds (each ≥ 0.945); otherwise the better 5-member wins. Label-v2 swaps are not part of E-ref (fold 0 cannot read label changes; may be a later row). Parity: each kernel is checked on the public test run (completes, members listed, finite probabilities) before submission | **E1 (L4 + P1-cnx): LB 0.943** (submission 57234306) = L4 0.943 → tie band, **L4 kept** (lean), P1-cnx not added. **E2 (L4 + B2): LB 0.944** (submission 57234312) ≥ 0.944 → **B2 earns a place; E2 (L4 + B2, 5 members) is the new ensemble base and best LB.** +0.001 over L4 is inside the LB SD (≈ 0.003), so this is a rule-pass, not a demonstrated gain; Model B stays closed for new training (B3 not run) but B2 weights stay in use. 6-member L4 + cnx + B2 not tried (needs each arm ≥ 0.945; E1 was 0.943) |
| T-pre | **Text-alignment pre-checks (CPU, $0, no training, no label changed).** User request 2026-10-15 ("do the prechecks") after brainstorming a training-only text-alignment loss on the 1e recipe (frozen report encoder; image side = 1e per-finding features; inference unchanged). Question: do report embeddings carry finding information in a form an image encoder could usefully align to? Reports are multilingual (langdetect on 4,407: en 1,736, es 682, tr 546, hr 406, el 321, de 262, bg 220, nl 153, fr 81), so language ≈ site is a shortcut risk. Data: training folds 1–5 only (3,524 studies; fold 0 and gold-58 not read); probe targets = label-v2 confident cells (≥ 0.7 vs ≤ 0.15, mid excluded), targets with ≥ 30 per class; probes = L2 logistic regression, CV by the existing folds 1–4 (fold 5 always train). Encoders (local CPU, untrusted downloads in `data/pretrained_text/`, safetensors or `weights_only=True`, no report leaves the machine): TF-IDF char 2–5-grams (lexical baseline, no download); `intfloat/multilingual-e5-base` (MIT); `UCSD-VA-health/RadBERT-RoBERTa-4m` (Apache-2.0, English only). Checks: **C1 coverage** = per target, share of studies with a non-empty HPO-v3.1 rule-evidence span (`rule_evidence.csv`), i.e. per-finding text exists (variant B). **C2 whole-report probe** macro AUC per encoder (RadBERT and e5 also on the English subset). **C3 language shortcut**: between-language share of embedding variance; leave-one-language-out probe (train on 8 languages, test on the 9th, embeddings centred per language) vs in-language CV; language × scanner-manufacturer Cramér's V (`study_header_tags.csv`). **C4 per-finding span probe** (variant B): e5 embeddings of the evidence span, POS vs NEG among mentioned cells (tests negation handling). Prior evidence: P1-sev (report severity aux heads) gave noise-level change; 6-ext (40 extra report findings) never trained. Script `scripts/text_alignment_prechecks.py` → `data/text_alignment_prechecks/` | **Pre-registered 2026-10-15, before any embedding is computed.** GO to a GPU spec (shown with $ before any launch) needs all of: e5 whole-report probe macro AUC ≥ 0.90 (C2); e5 leave-one-language-out macro AUC ≥ 0.85 (C3; else the text space is language-bound and alignment would teach site, NO-GO unless the user approves local translation); e5 not below TF-IDF by more than 0.01 (C2; if lexical matches or beats it, a neural text target adds nothing over tags, NO-GO for a text encoder). Variant choice: B (per-finding) only if ≥ 6 of 12 targets have coverage ≥ 30% (C1) and the span probe is ≥ 0.90 (C4); otherwise A (whole report). RadBERT is reported but cannot be the main encoder (covers 39% of studies). A GO is not a launch: the user still chooses text alignment vs Phase 3 | **NO-GO** (2026-10-15, `data/text_alignment_prechecks/summary.txt`; 2 of 3 gates fail). C2: e5 whole-report macro 0.9435 (≥ 0.90 passes) but **TF-IDF 0.9647 beats it by 0.021** (fail); English subset TF-IDF 0.9615 / e5 0.9481 / RadBERT 0.9412, so the radiology model is no better than generic e5 or lexical. C3: **46% of e5 embedding variance is between-language**; leave-one-language-out (centred) **0.8407 < 0.85** (fail; raw 0.8054, TF-IDF 0.8134) vs 0.9399 in-language; language × scanner model Cramér's V **0.78** (manufacturer 0.42), i.e. text-space language structure is mirrored by a channel the image encoder can see → site-shortcut risk confirmed. C1: coverage ≥ 0.30 on 10/12 targets (Synovitis 0.12, Fracture 0.20). C4: span probe 0.9939 (11 targets) — negation handled, but spans come from the same rules that set the labels, so this is close to circular. Context: P1-sev (report severity heads) was already noise-level. **Text alignment with a frozen report encoder closed**; not revisited unless local translation to one language is approved (not planned). Cost $0 |
| T-pre2 | **Text alignment, follow-up: is there anything in the report embedding beyond the labels? (CPU, $0, no training, no label changed).** User request 2026-10-15 ("yeah do it") after T-pre's NO-GO; T-pre's TF-IDF gate only measured the existing labels, and a same-language-negatives contrastive loss could remove the cross-language shortcut by design. Same data as T-pre (training folds 1–5, 3,524 studies; fold 0 and gold-58 not read; cached e5 report embeddings). **R1 variance:** fold-CV R² of the e5 embedding from (a) language one-hot, (b) known label side = label-v2 probabilities + HPO-v3.1 rule-state one-hots (SIL / NEG / BELOW / POS / BORD / HEDGE / LIKELY per target), (c) both + language × label interactions. Residual = embedding minus the in-sample linear fit on (c) (zero linear information about labels, rule states or language mean). **R2 beyond-label content:** extra-finding tags outside the 12 targets, found by a hand-written lexicon with a simple preceding-negation rule, in the 5 Latin-script languages I can write reliably (en / es / de / fr / nl, 2,335 studies; tr / hr / el / bg excluded from tag evaluation, not from the residual fit): posterior horn, bucket handle, chondromalacia, osteophyte, tendinopathy, loose body, meniscal extrusion, parameniscal cyst / ganglion, Hoffa, plica; a tag is evaluated if ≥ 30 positives. Probe = L2 logistic regression, CV on folds 1–4; residual vs label side alone vs raw e5. **R3 within-language site channel:** residual → scanner model (`study_header_tags.csv`), one-vs-rest AUC per (language, model) with ≥ 30 studies, macro. Script `scripts/text_alignment_beyond_labels.py` → `data/text_alignment_prechecks/beyond_labels/` | **Pre-registered 2026-10-15, before the script is written or run.** GO to a GPU spec for option 1 (same-language-negative contrastive, per-finding, silent masked, on 1e; spec + $ shown before any launch) needs both: **R2 residual macro AUC ≥ 0.80 over ≥ 4 evaluable tags** (the embedding carries finding content the labels do not) and **R3 residual site macro AUC ≤ 0.75** (no strong within-language site channel left). R2 pass + R3 fail → NO-GO for now (content exists but the site channel survives within language; would need protocol-text stripping first). R2 fail → text alignment closed for good. R1 is descriptive. A GO is not a launch; the user still chooses vs Phase 3 | **NO-GO for now** (2026-10-15, `data/text_alignment_prechecks/beyond_labels/summary.txt`): R2 passes, R3 fails. R1: fold-CV R² language 0.434, labels + rule states 0.230, both + interactions 0.562; residual 38% of variance. **R2 residual macro 0.857 over 10 tags** (label side + language 0.830, raw e5 0.959): hoffa 0.955, chondromalacia 0.951, bucket handle 0.939, parameniscal cyst 0.900, tendinopathy 0.899, plica 0.871, extrusion 0.827, osteophyte 0.771, loose body 0.745, posterior horn 0.714 → the embedding carries finding content the labels do not. Caveat: chondromalacia / bucket handle / plica positives are ≥ 80% English, so part of R2 may be residual language structure (English-only re-check not run: it cannot change the decision). **R3 residual within-language scanner-model macro 0.782 > 0.75** (31 language–model pairs; English sites strongest, e.g. Vantage 0.986, Aera (MAGNETOM) 0.959; label side alone 0.761, raw e5 0.815) → a within-language site / template channel survives. Text alignment parked: would need protocol / technique text stripped and R3 re-run as a new row before any GPU spec; not planned before the deadline. Cost $0 |
| T-pre3 | **Text, follow-up 2: site-stripped alignment target (arm B) and extra-finding tag heads (arm A) — CPU checks, $0, no training, no label changed.** User request 2026-10-15 ("we are missing out on text alignment … do the CPU checks") after T-pre2 (content beyond labels 0.857, but within-language site channel 0.782). Same data as T-pre / T-pre2 (training folds 1–5, 3,524 studies; fold 0 and gold-58 not read; cached e5 embeddings; no raw report text is printed or reviewed by me — only counts and scores). **Site regressors** = language one-hot + (language × scanner model) one-hot (combos < 10 studies pooled per language) + manufacturer + field strength. **T_full** = e5 embedding minus its in-sample linear fit on the site regressors (candidate arm-B target, keeps label content). **T_beyond** = embedding minus the fit on site regressors + T-pre2's label side (labels, rule states, language × labels). **S-R3 site check:** within-language scanner-model one-vs-rest AUC (pairs with ≥ 30 studies) on raw e5, T_full and T_beyond, with a linear probe and a kNN probe (k = 25, cosine; the kNN tests the similarity structure a contrastive loss uses). **S-R2 content check:** T-pre2's 10 lexicon tags (en / es / de / fr / nl) from T_beyond, all 5 languages and English only. **A-tags (arm A quality):** per tag positives, share of matches negated, lift = P(related target ≥ 0.7 | tag) / P(related target ≥ 0.7) for tags with an expected relation (posterior horn / bucket handle / parameniscal cyst → either meniscus; extrusion → Medial Meniscus or Medial OA; chondromalacia → PF OA; osteophyte, loose body → any OA; tendinopathy, Hoffa, plica: no relation, not lift-tested), and tag × (language, scanner model) Cramér's V (combos ≥ 30). Script `scripts/text_alignment_site_stripped.py` → `data/text_alignment_prechecks/site_stripped/` | **Pre-registered 2026-10-15, before the script is written or run.** **Arm B GO** needs all: T_full site macro AUC ≤ 0.65 with both linear and kNN probes; T_beyond tag macro ≥ 0.80 over ≥ 4 tags; English-only T_beyond tag macro ≥ 0.75 over tags with ≥ 30 English positives (guards T-pre2's language caveat). **Arm A tag kept** if ≥ 40 positives, lift ≥ 1.3 where a relation is expected, and site Cramér's V < 0.40; **arm A GO** if ≥ 5 tags kept (studies in tr / hr / el / bg masked for the aux heads). A GO leads to a GPU spec (1e recipe + arm A and/or arm B, run in sequence, $ shown before any launch); each arm is judged on the LB | **Arm A GO (6 tags), arm B NO-GO with e5-base** (2026-10-15, `data/text_alignment_prechecks/site_stripped/summary.txt`). Variance kept: T_full 0.408, T_beyond 0.323. S-R3 site (raw e5 linear 0.815 / kNN 0.809): **T_full kNN 0.737 > 0.65 (fail)**, T_beyond kNN 0.716. **Design flaw, recorded honestly:** the linear site gate is uninformative — T_full linear 0.097 and T_beyond 0.094 are far *below* 0.5 because site means were removed in-sample on all studies, so in every CV split the held-out rows are shifted opposite to the training rows and a linear probe learns a flipped rule (≈ 0.90 if flipped). Only the kNN gate is meaningful; it alone blocks arm B, so no decision changes. S-R2 after site stripping: macro 0.803 over 10 tags (passes, barely; T-pre2 0.857), English-only 0.799 (passes) — bucket handle 0.937, hoffa 0.925, parameniscal cyst 0.890, chondromalacia 0.889, tendinopathy 0.826, extrusion 0.795, osteophyte 0.768, loose body 0.734, plica 0.674, posterior horn 0.598 (part of its T-pre2 signal was site style). A-tags: **kept** bucket handle (50 pos, lift 2.25, site V 0.14), chondromalacia (133, 1.72, 0.37), osteophyte (202, 2.61, 0.24), loose body (43, 2.64, 0.10; 58% of matches negated, so the negation rule matters), meniscal extrusion (225, 2.13, 0.27), parameniscal cyst / ganglion (214, 1.44, 0.22); **dropped** for site V ≥ 0.40: posterior horn (0.47), tendinopathy (0.51), Hoffa (0.41), plica (0.54). Next: arm-B encoder bake-off (row T-enc); arm A GPU spec (1e + 6 masked aux heads, en / es / de / fr / nl studies only) to be shown with $ before any launch. Cost $0 |
| T-enc | **Text encoder bake-off for arm B (embeddings on Kaggle T4, checks on CPU, $0, no training, no label changed).** User request 2026-10-15 ("is multilingual e5 the best one … if its fast with a GPU use a GPU"). Kernel `tumis001/rsna-text-encoder-bakeoff` (`kaggle/submission_kernels/text_encoder_bakeoff/`) reads the competition `train.csv` inside Kaggle (report text never leaves Kaggle; output = embeddings + study ids only), skips gold rows, embeds whole reports with sentence-transformers, fp16: `BAAI/bge-m3` (MIT, max 2,048 tokens), `sentence-transformers/LaBSE` (Apache-2.0, translation-aligned, 512), `intfloat/multilingual-e5-large` (MIT, 512, "passage: "), plus `multilingual-e5-base` as a parity check against the local T-pre cache (mean cosine ≥ 0.99 required, else the bake-off is void). `Qwen/Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B` (Apache-2.0, LLM-derived embedder, 2,048, fp32 because bf16-trained on a fp16-only T4; added with the user's OK 2026-10-15, kernel v2; v1 without it superseded). Among eligible encoders (e5-base included, from T-pre3), the arm-B encoder is the one with the highest T_beyond tag macro (tie within 0.005 → higher leave-one-language-out). No eligible encoder → arm B NO-GO for all. Descriptive only, not gated: label probe, LOLO, language variance. **Amendment 2026-10-15 (before any encoder is scored; user: "do the scoring on kaggle gpu if that can speed up"):** scoring moves to a Kaggle T4 kernel `tumis001/rsna-text-encoder-bakeoff-score` (`kaggle/submission_kernels/text_encoder_bakeoff_score/`) that re-embeds all five encoders in-kernel (same settings; Qwen3 batch 2) and runs the same probes re-implemented in PyTorch (standardise on the outer-train split; L2 logistic regression, same objective as scikit-learn, C ∈ {0.01, 0.1, 1, 10} picked by 3-fold stratified inner CV on AUC then refit; kNN k = 25 cosine, uniform vote). Inputs uploaded as private dataset `tumis001/rsna-text-bakeoff-inputs` (folds, label-v2, rule states, scanner header columns, language calls, local e5-base cache; no report text). **Parity gate:** GPU e5-base must reproduce the CPU scikit-learn values within ± 0.005 (T-pre label probe 0.9435 and LOLO 0.8407; T-pre3 raw / T_full / T_beyond site and tag values once T-pre3 finishes) and the in-kernel e5-base must match the local cache (mean cosine ≥ 0.99); otherwise the GPU scores are void and the CPU script `scripts/text_encoder_bakeoff.py` decides | **Arm B NO-GO for all encoders** (2026-10-16, `data/kaggle_kernel_outputs/text_encoder_bakeoff_score_v1/summary.json`; kernel 4 h 3 min on a T4). **Parity passed:** GPU vs CPU on the local e5-base cache — label probe −0.0001, LOLO −0.0004, language variance +0.0004, raw site linear +0.0007 / kNN 0.0000; vs T-pre3 — T_full site linear 0.099 (CPU 0.097), kNN 0.737 (0.737), tags all-5 0.798 (0.803, −0.0048), English 0.795 (0.799); in-kernel e5-base vs cache mean cosine 1.000. GPU scores therefore stand. **Per encoder** (label probe / LOLO / language variance / T_full site kNN / T_beyond tags all-5 / English): e5-base 0.942 / 0.840 / 0.460 / 0.738 / 0.800 / 0.795; LaBSE 0.927 / 0.842 / 0.293 / 0.753 / 0.717 / 0.726; e5-large 0.954 / 0.879 / 0.425 / 0.743 / 0.797 / 0.785; bge-m3 0.956 / 0.874 / 0.272 / 0.738 / 0.754 / 0.767; Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B 0.962 / 0.887 / 0.345 / **0.717** / 0.783 / 0.804. Every encoder fails the kNN site gate (0.717–0.753 vs ≤ 0.65); the linear site gate (0.10–0.13) is the in-sample artifact recorded in T-pre3 and decides nothing. Reading: better encoders carry more label content (Qwen3 label probe 0.962, close to TF-IDF 0.965; LOLO +0.046 over e5-base) and lower between-language variance (bge-m3, LaBSE), but none removes the within-language scanner-style neighbourhood that a contrastive loss would pull image features towards. Arm B closed; arm A (6 lexicon tags) is unaffected. Cost $0 (Kaggle quota) |

SWA 21–25, no TTA: **clear6 0.9680** (adopt needs ≥ 0.9725; miss −0.0045), **10-target 0.9086** (≥ 0.9085, passes by 0.0001). vs 1e clear6 −0.0039 [−0.0098, +0.0015], 10-target +0.0013 [−0.0026, +0.0053]; vs 1e-s27 −0.0010 / −0.0011. | F0 | **Fusion diagnostic for a two-branch ResNet + Swin3D model (inference + CPU, ~$1, no training).** User idea 2026-10-16: one network with a 2D / 2.5D ResNet branch (1e) and a 3D Swin branch (B2) joined by shared per-finding attention / heads. Step 0 tests the frozen version on data neither model trained on: Modal H100 (`fusion_features`, `scripts/extract_fusion_features_gpu.py`) runs 1e (SWA 21–25, d96, 320, 94 windows) and B2 (SWA 11–15, d96n, 320) on fold 0 (825 studies, no TTA) and saves each finding's attention-pooled feature (2048-d / 768-d) and logit → `fusion_f0/`. CPU (`scripts/fusion_diagnostic.py`, single-threaded) on the Steven-v4 ruler cells, clear6 / 10-target exactly as `audit_phase1.py`, per finding, out-of-fold by 5-fold CV inside fold 0 × 5 repeats: **A** = mean of the two probabilities (E2-style); **S** = L2 logistic on the two logits (reweighting control); **F** = L2 logistic on the two logits + PCA-32 of each branch's feature (logits ×10 after standardising so the penalty acts on the features); Fr / Fs = one branch's features only. C ∈ {0.001, 0.01, 0.1, 1} by inner 3-fold CV. Paired bootstrap 2,000. Dry run with random features (pipeline check): A 0.9715 / 0.9112, S 0.9721 / 0.9095, F 0.9693 / 0.8972, i.e. useless features cost F ≈ −0.004 to −0.005, so a real pass needs real signal | **Pre-registered 2026-10-16, before any real feature is extracted.** **Parity gate:** each model's extracted logits must reproduce its `val_swa.json` (max |Δp| ≤ 0.01, corr ≥ 0.999), else void. **GO to Step 1** (a pre-registered joint two-branch model, ~$10 per run, spec + $ shown first) only if all of: F − A ≥ +0.004 on 10-target with bootstrap 95% CI lower bound > 0; F clear6 ≥ A clear6 − 0.001; F − S ≥ +0.002 on 10-target (gain from features, not from re-weighting). Otherwise **NO-GO**: the two-branch model is not built. Caveats fixed now: 825 studies is small, so F can understate what a jointly trained fusion learns; fold-0 fitting is diagnostic only, never used for a submission | **NO-GO: the two-branch model is not built** (2026-10-16, `data/coatnet_d96_fold0/fusion_f0/summary.txt`; Modal extraction ~$1). Parity passed (1e max |Δp| 0.0053, B2 0.0000, corr 1.000). clear6 / 10-target: 1e 0.9719 / 0.9073, B2 0.9542 / 0.8941, **A (average) 0.9715 / 0.9112**, S (reweight) 0.9721 / 0.9094, **F (feature fusion) 0.9639 / 0.8875**, Fr 0.9673 / 0.8959, Fs 0.9679 / 0.9030. **F − A 10-target −0.0128 [−0.0188, −0.0066]**, clear6 −0.0030; F − S −0.0118. Real features did *worse* than the random-feature dry run (F 0.8972): they fit fold-0 noise. F lost most on rare findings (Fracture −0.051, Contusion −0.045, Synovitis −0.017 vs A), gained only on MCL +0.007, Baker's +0.006, Medial OA +0.002. Plain averaging also beats learned reweighting (A 0.9112 vs S 0.9094), consistent with earlier reweighting results. Reading: at fold-0 scale there is no extra signal in the per-finding features beyond the two models' logits that a learned combiner can use; averaging already captures the complementarity. Caveat (fixed in advance): 825 studies understates what a combiner trained on 3,524 studies could learn. **Post-hoc review (2026-10-16, user question "was this a good test?"):** the test answers a narrower question than the proposal and is biased against it — (1) frozen, already-pooled per-finding features (each branch's own attention already chose its tokens), not combined attention over both token sets; (2) no co-adaptation, which is the point of joint training; (3) n ≈ 825 with rare findings at ~30–60 positives, and the dry run showed even useless features cost −0.004 to −0.005, so the +0.004 gate needed roughly +0.009 of real signal; (4) unsupervised PCA-32 may drop the discriminative directions, and inner CV often picked the weakest penalty (C = 1). **Valid conclusion:** late fusion / stacking of the frozen 1e and B2 features does not beat averaging. **Not established:** whether a jointly trained two-branch model with token-level combined attention beats E2 — inconclusive, not refuted |
| J1 | **Joint two-branch model: ResNet-50 (2D / 2.5D) + Swin3D-T (3D) with combined per-finding attention, trained end to end.** The proper test of the user's idea after F0's post-hoc review (F0 tested frozen late fusion only). New runner `scripts/joint_r50_swin_runner.py` (imports both runners unchanged; Modal phase `joint_j1`, profile `--joint-profile-run`). **ResNet branch** = Exp 1e exactly (resnet50.tv_in1k, d96 windows: 16 at 384 in training, 94 at 320 for evaluation, 1e augmentation; tokens = LayerNorm window features [K, 2048]). **Swin branch** = B2 exactly (Swin3D-T Kinetics-400 on d96n, patch embedding + stages 1–2 frozen, llrd 0.9, drop path 0.25, aug2; tokens = all stage-4 tokens + series embedding [N, 768]). Each branch keeps its own per-finding attention head (deep supervision). **Fusion:** each token set → Linear 512 + LayerNorm + branch-type embedding; per finding **one softmax over the union of both branches' tokens** (1e's tanh attention MLP), with each branch's scores offset by −log(its token count) + a learned per-finding branch gate (without it the ResNet tokens start at ~0.2% of the mass, ~16 vs ~8,000 tokens, and their share would shift between 16 training and 94 evaluation windows; found in the CPU smoke test) → pooled 512-d → per-finding linear logit. **Branch dropout** p 0.15 per study (one random branch's tokens removed from the fused pool). **Loss** = fused BCE + 0.5 × (ResNet-head BCE + Swin-head BCE). **Labels** label-v2 (latD2, sha dd0ab9ab…) for every head; validation labels unchanged. **Optimiser** AdamW wd 0.05: ResNet backbone 1e-4, ResNet head 1e-3, Swin groups as B2 (5e-5 top, decay 0.9; head 1e-3), fusion 1e-3; 2 studies per forward × 4 accumulation = 8 per step; one cosine (1 warm-up epoch, 1% floor) over 20 epochs; grad clip 1.0; bf16; seed 2026. **Primary model** SWA 16–20 + BN refresh of the ResNet branch (256 training studies), 320, no TTA. Scored at epochs 1, 3, 5, 10, 13, 15–20 (fused, both heads, mean of heads, val BCE, ResNet attention share per finding). Early stop if fused val12 < 0.83 at epoch 5 (1e 0.858, B2 0.866 there). Profile first (`joint_profile`, ~$1): parity of the trained 1e / B2 weights loaded into the joint model (≤ 0.01 vs their val_swa.json on 40 studies), gradient in every part, both dropout paths, timing and memory; the training launch waits for the measured cost and an explicit go | **Pre-registered 2026-10-16, before the profile or any training.** Scorer `scripts/audit_joint_j1.py` (cells, clear6 and 10-target as `audit_phase1.py`; paired bootstrap 2,000). **PRIMARY** (does combined attention add anything over the same two branches averaged?): fused − mean(own branch heads) ≥ +0.003 on 10-target with 95% CI lower bound > 0, and clear6 not lower by > 0.001. **SUBMISSION-WORTHY:** fused 10-target ≥ A + 0.003, where A = mean(standalone 1e, B2) = 0.9715 / 0.9112 (F0), and fused clear6 ≥ A − 0.001; only then a Kaggle inference kernel is built (single model, two branches). **LB candidate gate** as phase 1: clear6 ≥ 0.9745 AND 10-target ≥ 0.9085. Diagnostics (no selection): each branch head vs its standalone model (does joint training hurt or help a branch?), per-target AUC, fused ResNet share per finding, curves. Prior: F0 found no stacking signal in the frozen features, and B2 alone is weaker (0.9542 / 0.8941), so a small or null effect is plausible; a PRIMARY FAIL with SUBMISSION-WORTHY FAIL closes two-branch fusion for this competition | **FAIL on all three gates (2026-10-16; `run_joint_j1/audit_j1.txt`; Modal ~$15 incl. profile and a ~$0.70 duplicate container).** SWA 16–20, clear6 / 10-target: **fused 0.9725 / 0.9084**; ResNet head 0.9727 / 0.8995; Swin head 0.9548 / 0.8953; mean of heads 0.9701 / 0.9071; A = mean(1e, B2) 0.9715 / 0.9112. **PRIMARY FAIL:** fused − mean of heads 10-target +0.0013 [−0.0011, +0.0037] (clear6 +0.0024 [+0.0000, +0.0055]). **SUBMISSION-WORTHY FAIL:** fused − A 10-target −0.0027 [−0.0062, +0.0009], clear6 +0.0010. **LB gate FAIL** (clear6 0.9725 < 0.9745; 10-target 0.9084 vs 0.9085). Side comparison (label-matched, not a gate): fused vs label-v2 r50 −0.0013 / +0.0010; vs mean(label-v2, B2) 0.9713 / 0.9117: +0.0012 / −0.0032. Diagnostics: (1) the fused head is a per-finding gated mixture (one softmax over the token union = branch mass × each branch's own softmax; no token-to-token interaction); the ResNet share is near-static per finding (sd 0.06–0.10 across studies; ACL 0.63, MCL 0.31, fracture 0.30). (2) **Branch competition:** the ResNet head lost 10-target −0.0080 [−0.0127, −0.0034] vs the label-v2 r50 (same labels), concentrated in the findings routed to Swin (MCL 0.751 vs 0.794, fracture 0.911 vs 0.920); Swin head = B2 (+0.0012). Fused vs mean(label-v2, B2) per finding: MCL −0.025, fracture −0.012; ACL, Medial OA, PF OA, effusion +0.002 to +0.004. (3) Fused − mean of heads by head-disagreement tercile: low −0.0015, mid +0.0018, high +0.0007 (no sign that disagreement is resolved better). (4) Fused − mean of heads grew in the low-LR tail (epochs 10–13 ≈ 0, 15–20 +0.0003 to +0.0017). Fold 0 (1,000 paired bootstraps): **E3 clear6 0.9764 / 10-target 0.9185 (fit LB 0.945); vs L4 +0.0022 [−0.0003, +0.0048] / −0.0015 [−0.0037, +0.0005]; vs E2 +0.0027 [+0.0004, +0.0054] / −0.0021 [−0.0041, −0.0001]** | **Pre-registered 2026-10-16, before the kernel is built or run.** One submission, judged on public LB vs E2 0.944 / L4 0.943 (LB SD ≈ 0.003; the unrounded rank inside a tie block reported as a secondary signal). **≥ 0.945** → E3 becomes the ensemble base and best LB. **0.943–0.944** → tie; E3 is adopted as the lean base (one preprocessing pipeline, 4 members vs E2's 3 pipelines, 5 members), E2 stays the best-LB reference. **≤ 0.942** → rejected; E2 kept. | J1-XAI | **What J1 learned — instrumented fold-0 inference + CPU ablations (diagnostic, no submission).** User request 2026-10-16 ("what went wrong in the J1 training… ablations, XAI analyses"). `scripts/joint_j1_xai.py` via `modal_coatnet_d96.py --joint-xai-run` (1× H100, ~10 min, ≈ $1.5): J1 SWA 16–20 and the label-v2 r50 reference (sha 1c6006b1…, uploaded to the volume as `ref_labelv2/`) on the 825 fold-0 studies; per study and finding the fused / R / S / lv2 logits, fused ResNet mass, within-branch fused logits; per window and per Swin token the fused attention score and contribution plus each head's own (exact CPU recomputation of the fused head: branch-only, gate sweeps, window / slot / series occlusion); per-finding pooled features of J1-R and lv2 (linear probes); exact CAMs of each finding's top-8 windows.
