Validation labels used only for scoring on CPU, never for changing anything | **Pre-registered 2026-10-16.** Questions, each answered with a number: (1) is the fused loss vs the 50/50 branch average a routing problem (an oracle per-finding gate on fold 0 recovers ≥ half the gap) or a feature problem (it does not); (2) did the ResNet branch lose MCL / fracture information (J1-R vs lv2 pooled-feature probe, 5-fold within fold 0) or only head calibration; (3) where the fused head looks (slot / series / depth mass) vs lv2 and the standalone heads. Outcome feeds a J1b proposal only if (1) or (2) points to a specific fix; no training without a separate pre-registration and approval | **Done 2026-10-16** (95 s on 1× H100; parity vs run: fused 0.0025, R head 0.0038, S head 0.0024, share 0.0019; outputs `data/coatnet_d96_fold0/joint_j1_xai/`, analysis.txt). **(1) Not a routing problem:** per-study routing ≈ a constant per-finding mass (0.9722 / 0.9083 vs fused 0.9725 / 0.9084; per-study sd of the ResNet mass 0.05–0.10); the oracle per-finding gate (fit on fold 0) reaches 0.9726 / 0.9097, recovering 0.0013 of the 0.0033 10-target gap to avg(lv2, B2) (0.9713 / 0.9117) = 39% < half → **feature problem**. The fused head is already better than averaging its own heads (0.9701 / 0.9071). **(2) The ResNet branch is the weak part:** J1 R head 0.9728 / 0.8995 vs lv2 0.9739 / 0.9075 (−0.008 10-target; MCL −0.043, Lateral OA −0.015, ACL −0.011, Fracture −0.009); the Swin head is not (0.9548 / 0.8953 vs B2 0.9542 / 0.8941). Ridge probe (5-fold inside fold 0) on attention-pooled features J1R / lv2: MCL 0.758 / 0.797, ACL 0.882 / 0.899, rest equal; window-mean features MCL 0.750 / 0.745 → MCL information is in the windows but J1-R does not pool it: attention spread over 50 effective windows vs lv2 34, coronal-fluid mass 0.25 vs 0.34, CAM correlation with lv2 on shared top windows 0.46 (ACL 0.76, meniscus 0.81). Consistent with MCL starvation (gate −0.36 towards Swin; removing the ResNet coronal-fluid windows from the fused pool costs MCL −0.001, the Swin coronal series −0.023). Confound: J1's ResNet trained 20 epochs at 0.5× aux weight vs 1e/lv2 25 epochs at 1×. **(3) Where it looks:** fused ACL and meniscus run on ResNet sagittal fluid (occlusion ACL −0.025); MCL on Swin coronal; effusion / contusion / fracture partly on Swin axial (−0.007 / −0.009 / −0.015); the Swin S head's fracture attention sits on peripheral grid cells (inner 6×6 mass 0.34 vs 0.60 for the other findings), the fused head's does not. **Complementarity:** joint training made the branches no more different than separately trained models (Spearman J1R–J1S vs lv2–B2 within ±0.016 on every finding). Reading: a J1b that fixes the ResNet branch (full-weight aux, 25 epochs, or stop-gradient) could at best approach avg(lv2, B2) in one network; whether that is worth training depends on that pair's LB, which has not been measured. No J1b without a separate pre-registration |
| X0 | **Cross-finding synergy check: does a combiner that sees all 12 findings beat averaging? (CPU, $0, no submission).** User request 2026-10-16 ("go beyond weighted average … the sum should be larger" → "do the checks"). Members' fold-0 logits: set **E3** (lv2, 4net, 1b, cnx; 48 inputs) and set **P** (lv2, B2; 24 inputs, the J2-X pair). Combiners, each per finding k, as a residual on the members' mean logit m_k (Δ = 0 is exactly averaging in logit space): **C-own** Δ from finding k's member logits only (learned reweighting, control); **C-cross** Δ from all findings' mean logits (12) + finding k's member logits; **C-quad** C-cross + pairwise products of the 12 mean logits. L2 logistic regression (numpy Newton) on **label-v2 soft targets with their weights** (the training labels, not the ruler); λ by inner 4-fold CV on label-v2 weighted BCE from {0.1, 1, 10, 100, 1000}. Outer: 5-fold CV inside fold 0 × 5 seeds (predictions averaged over seeds), so every scored prediction is out-of-fold for its combiner. Scored with clear6 / 10-target on the Steven-v4 ruler (unchanged); paired study bootstrap 2,000, seed 0 | **Pre-registered 2026-10-16.** **SYNERGY** if the best combiner of C-cross / C-quad beats logit averaging of the same set by ≥ +0.003 10-target with bootstrap 95% CI lower bound > 0 and clear6 not lower by more than 0.001 (best-of-2 noted as a selection). C-own is the control: if C-own gains as much, the gain is reweighting, not cross-finding. **SYNERGY** → cross-finding inputs go into J2-X's Δ head. Otherwise → J2-X's Δ stays per finding; no ensemble change either way (a stacker would need cross-fitted members to be submitted) | **NO SYNERGY** (2026-10-16, `scripts/check_cross_finding_x0.py` → `data/coatnet_d96_fold0/check_x0_cross_finding.txt`). Δ vs logit mean, clear6 / 10-target [95% CI]: **E3** (logit mean 0.9764 / 0.9192): C-own −0.0002 / −0.0015 [−0.0023, −0.0007]; C-cross −0.0007 / −0.0022 [−0.0033, −0.0011]; C-quad −0.0009 / −0.0025 [−0.0040, −0.0011]. **P** (lv2 + B2, 0.9711 / 0.9116): C-own +0.0025 [+0.0001, +0.0053] / −0.0002 (a reweighting towards lv2 on the clear band, not synergy); C-cross −0.0001 / −0.0016; C-quad −0.0010 / −0.0019. Cross-finding information in the members' outputs adds nothing over averaging and the extra inputs cost (fracture −0.009 for E3). → J2-X's Δ head stays per finding; any synergy has to come from token-level interaction, not from output stacking |
| J2-X | **Joint ResNet + Swin3D with a synergy residual: final = ½(z_R + z_S).detach() + Δ, Δ from anatomy-aligned cross-attention.** User goal 2026-10-16: "go beyond the output of weighted averaged, the sum should be large[r than] the individual models combined". Built on J1-XAI (J1 = mixture of two pooled vectors, no token interaction; ResNet branch starved on MCL) and X0 (output-level cross-finding stacking adds nothing → Δ per finding, synergy must come from tokens). `scripts/j2x_runner.py`. Branches: ResNet-50 = 1e recipe on label-v2 (16 windows at 384 / 94 at 320, 25 epochs, own full loss); Swin3D-T = B2 recipe on label-v2 (own full loss, 15-epoch cosine then the 1% floor). Δ: each ResNet window token cross-attends (2 pre-norm blocks, 512-d, 8 heads) to the Swin stage-4 tokens of the same series at its slice depth ± 1 token (300 tokens + null; depth-offset and 10×10 cell embeddings), alignment exact from both manifests (series UID + native slice; shard-0 sample: 97.5% exact, coronal non-fluid windows have no Swin series → null only); per-finding attention pooling over windows → zero-initialised linear. Mirror drawn once per study for both branches. Loss BCE(final) + BCE(z_R) + BCE(z_S); BCE(final) reaches both backbones through Δ, not the branch heads. AdamW, Δ lr 1e-3; SWA 21–25 + BN refresh. Profile first (~$1: alignment coverage, mapping self-check under augmentation, parity of 1e / B2 weights inside J2-X with Δ = 0, gradient paths, timing); full run only after its exact cost is shown and approved | **Pre-registered 2026-10-16, before the profile.** Fold 0, SWA, 320, no TTA, Steven-v4 ruler, paired bootstrap 2,000. **SYNERGY (primary):** final − ens (the same model's logit average of its own heads) ≥ +0.003 10-target, 95% CI lower bound > 0, clear6 not lower by > 0.001. **NO-STARVATION check:** ResNet head 10-target ≥ lv2 − 0.003 (≥ 0.9045) and Swin head ≥ B2 − 0.003 (≥ 0.8911). **SUBMISSION-WORTHY:** final 10-target ≥ avg(lv2, B2) + 0.003 (≥ 0.9147) and clear6 ≥ 0.9703. **LB CANDIDATE** (phase-1 single-model gate): clear6 ≥ 0.9745 and 10-target ≥ 0.9085. Kill: epoch-5 val final < 0.83. Δ's fold-0 gain (final − ens) logged every scored epoch. Not SYNERGY → the 2D/3D token-interaction route is closed; SYNERGY but not SUBMISSION-WORTHY → ensemble member screen only | **Profile OK** (2026-10-16, 81 s): eval windows matched to a Swin row sagFS 0.994 / sagT1 0.970 / corFS 1.000 / corT1 0.000 (no Swin series) / axial 0.900, all 0.846; training-mode mapping self-check (series dropout, stride 2, mirror) 791/1,024 windows matched, all within tolerance; parity with Δ = 0: 1e weights 0.0032, B2 weights 0.0000, final = logit average (1e-7); BCE(final) reaches both backbones and Δ, not the branch heads; 60.1M params (Δ module 8.0M), peak 14.6 GiB. Profile timing 0.425 s/study (J1's profile said 0.318, its run was ~3.4× faster) → expected ~3.5 h ≈ $20 on H100. Full run awaiting approval |

Same reading: the gain is the amount of positive evidence, not the map pattern. By state (logit / strength): SIL 0.893/0.895, MILD 0.854/0.875, UNC 0.899/0.831, UNSP 0.847/0.863. Per finding: MCL 0.697→0.793 (198 pairs), Lateral OA 0.741→0.817, Fracture 0.931→0.865, Medial OA 0.877→0.850. `data/attention_side_audit/attention_map_distance_labelv2.txt` **Breakdown (post-hoc, user "how many cases of unsure per target is it getting hurt", `scripts/audit_attention_map_breakdown.py`, all 12 findings, label-v2 judge):** total positive mass vs logit: 5,307 unsure cells, 1,049 judged pos / 3,107 neg, 27,288 pairs, fixed 1,089 / broken 595 (net +494), cases helped 629 / hurt 386. Net gain: medial meniscus +155 (mild), lateral OA +108 (silent), Baker's +84 (mild), lateral meniscus +74 (mild), contusion +66 (uncertain +49), ACL +30 (silent), MCL +21 (mild). Net loss: medial OA −37 (silent −24, mild −25), fracture −22 (mild −13), synovitis −13 (silent). Rank-mean(logit, mass): fixed 661 / broken 272 (net +389), helped 506 / hurt 236, same winners and losers, smaller losses. `data/attention_side_audit/attention_map_breakdown_labelv2.txt` |
| AM-1 | **Whole-score impact of the AM-0 evidence score (fold 0, CPU, $0, no training).** User 2026-10-17: "would you suggest that we go ahead with these changes? how much impact can it have on overall score of auc". AM-0 helped only within unsure cells; the score is applied to every study, so clear cells can lose. Evidence score = total positive CAM mass per (study, finding) from the Exp 1e maps (`attention_side_fold0.npz`). Variants fixed before running: **V1** 1e: rank-mean(1e, mass), all 12 findings; **V2** = V1 only on the 7 AM-0 net winners (medial / lateral meniscus, lateral OA, Baker's, contusion, ACL, MCL; chosen on the same fold, optimistic); **V3** E3 (label-v2 + 4-network + 1b + cnx, probability mean) blended per finding as (1 − w)·rank(E3) + w·rank(mass), w ∈ {0.25, 0.5}, all 12 (mass from 1e, the only maps we have; a submission would compute it from label-v2 r50). `scripts/audit_attention_map_impact.py` | Diagnostic | **Pre-registered 2026-10-17, before running.** Primary (user: label-v2 as judge): 12-target macro AUC on label-v2 confident cells (weight > 0, y ≤ 0.3 / ≥ 0.7), 2,000 paired study bootstraps vs the unchanged model. Secondary (LB yardstick): clear6 / 10-target on Steven-v4 and the LB fit −0.687 + 1.671 × clear6. **Worth a submission test** (label-v2 r50 maps in the E3 kernel) only if V3 gains ≥ +0.003 on the primary with ≥ 90% bootstraps and clear6 does not fall by > 0.002; V1 / V2 tell whether the single model gains || **NO (2026-10-17).** Sanity: 1e val_swa vs npz logit rank corr ≥ 0.963. Label-v2 12-target: 1e 0.9382 → V1 0.9388 (+0.0006, 66%), V2 0.9394 (+0.0012, 83%); E3 0.9485 → V3 w0.25 0.9484 (−0.0001, 47%), w0.5 0.9453 (−0.0032, 2%). Steven clear6: E3 0.9764 → w0.25 0.9773 (+0.0009, 85%; LB fit 0.945 → 0.946), 10-target −0.0006; V1 / V2 10-target −0.0025 / −0.0022 (3–5%). Mass alone 0.9311 (−0.007 vs 1e): the unsure-cell gain (AM-0) is diluted by clear-vs-clear pairs, where the logit (evidence for minus against) is better; inside E3 the gain vanishes. Per finding E3 → w0.25: lateral OA +0.005, PF OA +0.004, fracture −0.005. `data/attention_side_audit/attention_map_impact.txt` |
| MS1-L1 | **Does MS1 use the neighbouring slices? Slice-use test, inference only (no training).** User 2026-10-17: "do the lead 1 test". Mechanism under test: each d96 window packs stack slices [c − 1, c, c + 1] into RGB, MedSigLIP was pretrained on grey (R = G = B) MRI, and MS1's patch embedding (the only place channels can be told apart) got LR ~2.6e-6, so MS1 may read depth as colour, which would hurt thin structures. `scripts/eval_slice_use_gpu.py` (Modal `--ms1-l1-run`, one H100): fold 0 (825, 94 windows, no TTA, same centres) re-scored with trained weights under three inputs: **normal** [c − 1, c, c + 1] (reproduction), **grey** [c, c, c] (no depth), **wide** [c − 3, c, c + 3] (neighbours clamped inside the centre's series slot). Models: MS1 SWA 21–25 and the label-v2 r50 (`ref_labelv2/r50_labelv2_swa21_25.pt`, phase r50_pertarget) as the control. Channel reversal is not tested (the sagittal mirror augmentation reverses channels in training). ~20 min H100, ~$1.5. Scorer `scripts/audit_slice_use.py` | Diagnostic | **Pre-registered 2026-10-17, before launch.** Primary (user: label-v2 as judge): 12-target macro AUC on label-v2 confident cells; grey drop = grey − normal per model, 1,000 paired bootstraps. Sanity: normal reproduces each val_swa.json (per-target |Δ| ≤ 0.002). **Verdict:** r50 grey drop < 0.005 → not informative; MS1 grey ≥ normal + 0.003 → packing hurts MS1, grey / centre-init input next; MS1 drop ≤ 0.003 or ≤ ⅓ of r50's → depth unused, centre-init patch embedding with its own LR (T1 + T2) is the next run; MS1 drop ≥ ⅔ of r50's → depth used, lead 1 dropped, lead 2 (layer probe) next; else inconclusive. Secondary: thin4 (ACL, MCL, PF OA, lateral OA), Steven clear6 / 10-target, ranking change, wide input, per finding || **Done 2026-10-17: DEPTH USED BY MS1 → lead 1 dropped.** Modal app ap-Q6yUiuMlL6cB7O63VErm9A (~15 min H100). Sanity OK (normal vs val_swa per-target |Δ| ≤ 0.0005, rank corr 1.000). Label-v2 12-target normal / grey / wide: MS1 0.9301 / 0.9209 / 0.9213 (grey −0.0092 [−0.014, −0.005]); r50 0.9426 / 0.9304 / 0.9276 (grey −0.0121). MS1 drop = 0.76 of r50's (rule ≥ ⅔ → used). Thin4: MS1 grey −0.014 vs r50 −0.010 (MS1 relies on neighbours at least as much on thin structures). Steven clear6 grey: MS1 −0.0099, r50 −0.0091. Rank change from grey: MS1 0.058, r50 0.112. Per finding, MS1 grey losses: Baker's −0.038, MCL −0.024, ACL −0.016, medial OA −0.014, PF OA −0.012. Reading: the near-frozen patch embedding still lets MS1 use depth; the morphology gap is not the channel packing. `data/coatnet_d96_fold0/run_sig_lv2/l1_slice_use/audit_l1.txt` |
| LOC-0 | **Can Grad-CAM give pseudo-coordinates for a lumbar-1st-style localise → crop pipeline? (CPU, $0, existing fold-0 maps).** User 2026-10-17: linked the RSNA 2024 lumbar 1st-place solution (stage 1 localiser trained on annotated coordinates, stage 2 tight anatomy-specific crops + slice MIL) with the Grad-CAM crop idea → "do the checks". Plan under test: CAM peaks of confident positives → pseudo-coordinates → a localiser learns the anatomy (works on negatives / misses too) → fixed per-finding box. Data: Exp 1e exact CAM per window (`attention_side_fold0.npz`, 12 × 12 grid, 11.7 mm cells, full 140 mm field) + DICOM geometry (ipp / iop) + knee side (`laterality.json` tags, else sagittal geometry, as 2-side). Per finding and positive (label-v2 confident positive, user's judge), the top-evidence window in sagittal fluid (and coronal fluid) → peak = in-plane CAM centroid of that window + centre-slice position, in a knee frame (medial +, anterior +, superior +; mm from the slab centre). `scripts/audit_cam_localisation.py` | Diagnostic | **Pre-registered 2026-10-17, before running.** **Q1 (learnable target):** pass if, for ≥ 6 of 12 findings, the in-plane peak spread of positives (median distance to the finding median) ≤ 15 mm; AND side-fixed findings (MCL, medial meniscus, medial OA / lateral meniscus, lateral OA) put the peak slice on the correct side for ≥ 70% of positives; AND findings do not share one hot spot (median pairwise distance of finding medians ≥ 10 mm). **Q2 (zoom):** pass if the median per-window box holding 80% of the CAM mass is ≤ 60 mm a side (≥ 2× zoom of the 117 mm eval view). Both pass → free Kaggle run of the CAM kernel on the training folds (localiser targets). Diagnostics: same on confident negatives (does the model look at the same spot when it finds nothing?), anatomical sense (ACL central, PF OA anterior, Baker's posterior), fixed box per finding. Caveat: 11.7 mm cells limit precision; a CAM peak is the model's evidence, not annotated anatomy || **Done 2026-10-17: Q1 PASS, Q2 PASS.** Sagittal fluid, label-v2 positives: in-plane peak spread ≤ 15 mm for 10/12 findings (10.0–14.5 mm; contusion 17.3, fracture 19.4); side-correct MCL 88%, medial meniscus 98%, lateral meniscus 96%, medial OA 99%, lateral OA 97%; finding medians pairwise 37.8 mm (one pair 1.2 mm: contusion / fracture). Anatomy makes sense (knee frame, mm): ACL central / inferior (−8, −5, −13); menisci ±23 ML, posterior −10, inferior −13 to −18 (posterior horns); PF OA anterior +23 / superior +11; effusion anterior-superior (+23, +19, suprapatellar); Baker's medial-posterior (+23, −33); MCL +31 medial. Negatives' top window lies 9–27 mm away (ACL 26.7, lateral meniscus 27.2, PF OA 22.0): when it finds nothing the model looks elsewhere, so a crop must come from a localiser, not the study's own CAM. Q2: median per-window 80% box 46.7 mm (4 cells; ≈2.5× zoom of the 117 mm view; quantised at 11.7 mm). Fixed boxes without per-study localisation 69–108 mm (knee position in the field). Coronal fluid similar (spread 9.3–22.0 mm). Next per rule: CAM on training folds; must be out-of-fold (cross-fitted teachers), not in-fold 1e. `data/attention_side_audit/cam_localisation.txt`**Drill-down (user: "what about Lateral OA and synovitis"):** lateral OA sagittal fine (97% side-correct, spread 13.8 mm) but box 70 mm and coronal weak (76% side-correct, median only 7 mm lateral); 35 of 65 positives are also medial OA positive and their coronal peak is central (−2.5 mm, 71%); lateral-only −11.3 mm, 83%. Synovitis: 73 / 94 positives also effusion-positive (0 effusion-negative); peak 8 mm from effusion's (suprapatellar), same top sagittal window in 58% of co-positives; 1e logits synovitis vs effusion rank corr 0.905 (labels 0.893): the model reads synovitis as effusion, as the labels do. Coronal spread 16.8 mm (fails 15).**Synovitis literature + reports (user: "how should synovitis look like, can we do cropping according to evidence in literature"):** non-contrast MRI shows synovitis only by surrogates: effusion-synovitis (fluid distension + thickened lining / debris / fronds in the recesses) and Hoffa-synovitis (fat-pad hyperintensity on sagittal PD-FS; vs contrast MRI sens 71% / spec 55% infrapatellar, 88% / 30% intercondylar, MOST); contrast scores use 11 sites, lining 2–4 mm. Our reports (577 present, 363 mild): 537 synovitis sentences, 41% name effusion in the same sentence, suprapatellar 19%, posterior / popliteal 12%, debris / fronds 9%, Hoffa 2%, 71% no site; no contrast mentioned. Fold 0, label-v2: synovitis AUC within effusion-positive studies (73 pos / 37 neg) label-v2 r50 0.845, E3 0.877; the effusion output as the score gives 0.667 / 0.675, so the models carry synovitis signal beyond effusion. |

W_n) with the window attention held fixed, back-propagated to the final normed patch tokens (32 x 32 over 140 mm, 4.4 mm cells); map = positive part of gradient x activation, saved as column / row profiles; plus the MAP head's own latent-query attention (finding-agnostic). | MS1-CP | **Crop probe: does a crop chosen by MS1's own token maps add signal to MS1's whole-field view? (Modal H100 inference ~$3–5 + CPU probe).** User 2026-10-17: "do it" (step 1 of the MS1 global + local plan, after LOC-0-MS1 passed). Frozen MS1 (`run_sig_lv2/weights_swa21_25.pt`), all 4,349 labelled studies, 94 eval windows. Per finding: global g_n = MS1's own attention-pooled feature (logit = g_n . W_n + b_n); crops = the top-4 windows by MS1's per-finding attention, gradient x activation map centroid (as LOC-0-MS1) -> 128 px (46.7 mm) square of the 384 px image, all 3 slice channels, resized to 448 (3.5x zoom, 1.46 mm per token) -> frozen encoder -> MAP -> MS1 LayerNorm; c_n = attention-weighted mean of the 4. CPU (offline `rsna-label-ds` container): crop probe per finding as `audit_ssl_probe.py` (standardise, PCA-256 and L2 logistic on training folds, lambda on fold 1) -> fold-0 crop score s_n; **A** = MS1 fold-0 logit, **B** = 2-input logistic [MS1 logit, s_n] cross-fitted inside fold 0 (5 stratified splits x 20 repeats). Label-v2 confident cells for training and scoring. Crop locations on training folds come from MS1's in-sample maps; LOC-0-MS1 showed they mark anatomy, not label (pos-neg 2–4 mm). `scripts/extract_ms1_crop_features_gpu.py`, `modal_coatnet_d96.py --ms1-crops-run`, `scripts/audit_ms1_crop_probe.py` | Diagnostic | **Pre-registered 2026-10-17, before launch.** Gate: over ACL, Medial Meniscus, Lateral Meniscus, Medial OA, Contusion, Fracture (LOC-0-MS1 tight maps), mean AUC(B) - AUC(A) >= +0.005 with paired study-bootstrap (2,000) 95% lower bound > 0 -> **build MS1 global + local**, crops for findings with own gain >= +0.005; else **stop** (MS1 unchanged). Frozen features + linear probe understate a fine-tuned gain, so FAIL is a real stop and PASS a safe go. Descriptive: s_n alone, MS1's head on the crop (zero-shot), train-fold probes A' (g_n) vs B' (g_n + c_n; biased against crops since g_n is in-sample there), all 12 findings || **Done 2026-10-17 (1,877 s H100 ≈ $2; pooled vs forward logits max 0.007; fold-0 rank corr vs val_swa 1.000). FAIL on frozen features.** **Re-graded same day (user challenge): this rules out only a free gain from MS1's current encoder on crops; it does not test a crop branch trained on crops** (MS1 was fine-tuned at whole-field scale only, the probe is linear on one pooled vector, and training-fold crop features are in-sample, all biased against crops). The pre-registered 'FAIL is a real stop' overstated what a frozen probe shows. Gate mean B-A **-0.0013, 95% CI [-0.0046, +0.0019]** (excludes +0.005). Per finding B-A: ACL +0.0069 [-0.004, +0.019], medial meniscus +0.0011, lateral meniscus -0.0032, medial OA -0.0001, contusion +0.0009, **fracture -0.0133 [-0.030, -0.003]**; outside the gate **Baker's +0.0118 [+0.003, +0.022]** (only CI above 0), effusion -0.0016 [-0.003, -0.001], rest within ±0.002. The crop probe alone is below MS1 for every finding (e.g. medial meniscus 0.949 vs 0.965, lateral meniscus 0.855 vs 0.912) except Baker's (0.907 vs 0.904): at 3.5x zoom the frozen encoder sees less than, and mostly the same as, the whole field. MS1's head on the crop (zero-shot) 0.65–0.92. Lambda chose 0.1 (grid edge) for all findings. `data/coatnet_d96_fold0/run_sig_lv2/crop_probe/audit_crop_probe.txt` |
| MS1-GL | **MS1 trained on global + local views, fused by the CW1 cross-window reader (Modal H100, smoke ~$0.4 then full ~$12).** User 2026-10-17/18: "I want MS1 to train on both global+local views, and learn complementary signal from both"; fusion = CW1 (this morning's 0.956-notebook reader) instead of J1-style late mixing (J1 / J1-XAI: no token interaction, no synergy); "the global and local - crops should have same augmentation". From MS1 SWA weights. Global = MS1's windows (16 random / 94 eval). Local = per finding a 128 px (46.7 mm) box at the frozen-MS1 token-map peak on one of its top-4 attended windows (MS1-CP table; training: drawn by MS1 attention, centre jitter ±16 px = ±5.8 mm, about 1.3 MS1 token cells, kept by the user 2026-10-18 after `run_ms1_gl_preview/jitter_examples.png`; eval: all 4). **Augmentation: one draw per study shared by both views** (mirror with the crop centre flipped, rotation / scale / shift as one affine, gamma, gain); crops = theta [sA | p], i.e. a zoom of the augmented whole image at the anatomy point, sampled from the full slice; eval = MS1 resize and [sI | p]. Shared encoder; crop tokens get a learned scale embedding (zero init). Heads: **global** = MS1's head (aux); **local** = crop feature -> own LayerNorm (init MS1's) -> V of its finding (init MS1's W), attention-weighted over the finding's crops (aux); **fused** = CW1 reader: each image a token (Linear 1152->384 + LN, + view-type, series, slice-position embeddings; crops + finding and in-plane centre embeddings), 2-layer pre-norm transformer (8 heads, FFN 768, dropout 0.1, final LN), per-finding attention over all tokens -> classifier; no gate, no bypass. Loss = BCE(fused) + 0.5 (BCE(global) + BCE(local)). AdamW, layer decay 0.9 at 2.5e-5 (half MS1), old head 5e-4, new 1e-3, scale embedding 1e-4 (no decay); 1 warm-up epoch, cosine to 1% over 8 epochs, SWA 5–8; 1 study x 8 accumulation (28 images per study). Fold 0 at 2/4/6/8 + SWA: fused, global, local, fused without crop tokens, reader pooling mass on crops per finding; SWA also 6 x (16 windows + 12 top-1 crops) averaged. Gold not predicted. Kaggle inference needs the frozen-MS1 crop pass (later). `scripts/ms1_gl_runner.py`, `modal_coatnet_d96.py --ms1-gl-smoke / --ms1-gl-run`, `scripts/audit_ms1_gl.py` | Model | **Pre-registered 2026-10-18, before launch (supersedes the 2026-10-17 late-fusion draft, never launched).** Judge: label-v2 confident cells, fold 0, 12-target mean (MS1 0.9301). **Primary:** fused SWA (94 windows + 48 crops) - MS1 SWA >= +0.003, paired study-bootstrap 95% lower bound > 0 -> LB submission (single; then as MS1's replacement in an ensemble). **Attribution:** fused - same model's global head >= +0.002, lower bound > 0 -> zoom + interaction add complementary signal. Descriptive: global - MS1 (continued training / shared encoder), local alone, fused without crop tokens, crop pooling mass, 6-pass average. If fused is still rising at epoch 8, that reads as "longer run", not fail. Smoke gate: peak memory < 75 GB and train <= 0.40 s/study, else adjust before the full run || **Smoke 2026-10-18: PASS.** Peak 38.8 GB (train and eval); train 0.386 s/study averaged over 32 studies including start-up, ~0.31 steady (steps 9–32); eval 0.459 s/study (94 windows + 48 crops, 16 studies). Start losses fused / global / local 0.703 / 0.373 / 0.523 (fresh reader at ~ln 2; global = MS1 level); scale embedding moving off zero. **Full run 2026-10-18: FAIL (both gates).** 8 epochs x 18.0 min, eval 0.405 s/study, 38.8 GB, ~3.1 h. Confident cells, 12-target, SWA 5-8: MS1 0.9301, fused 0.9269, global 0.9317, local 0.9010, fused without crops 0.9238, 6-pass 0.9262. fused - MS1 -0.0031 [-0.0082, +0.0017]; fused - global -0.0047 [-0.0109, +0.0012]; global - MS1 +0.0016 [-0.0003, +0.0036]. Curve (fused - MS1): epoch 4 -0.0115, 6 -0.0050, SWA -0.0031 (still rising); fused val BCE 0.528 / 0.523 / 0.500 / 0.491 at 2 / 4 / 6 / 8. **Diagnosis:** (1) MCL (16 positives) collapses in the reader: fused 0.861, without crops 0.868, global 0.940 -> -0.079 on one finding = -0.0066 on the mean; the reader, trained from scratch, never learned MCL pooling. (2) Crops help inside the reader: fused - fused without crops +0.0031 (12) / **+0.0041 [+0.0020, +0.0064] (11, no MCL; post hoc, descriptive)**; per finding fracture +0.016, ACL +0.007, lateral OA +0.003, medial meniscus +0.002 vs global. (3) The reader without crops pools worse than MS1's head (-0.0021 on 11), which eats about half the crop gain. 11 targets without MCL: fused - MS1 +0.0024 [-0.0010, +0.0058], fused - global +0.0020 [-0.0013, +0.0056]. Read: the zoom carries complementary signal; the from-scratch fusion head loses it. Next variant: residual fusion (fused = global logit + reader correction, correction zero-init) |
| r50-GL | **The label-v2 r50 recipe trained on global windows + per-finding zoomed crops, fused by a residual reader (Modal H100, smoke ~$0.5 then full ~$6–8).** User 2026-10-18: "can r50 use the global+local logic", "rethink for r50, don't be hasty with the experiment setup", design approved ("sounds good"). | r50-GL v3 | **One r50 that reads the whole study and per-finding zoomed regions, each finding's answer a learned per-study mix of its two views. Each change answers a diagnosed v2 failure.** User 2026-10-18: "okay lets start v3" (after: single-model improvement, fix the diagnosed mechanism, no averaging / frozen / two-backbone designs). `scripts/r50_gl3_runner.py` (Modal `--r50-gl3-smoke` / `--r50-gl3-run`, out `run_r50_gl3`). Base = label-v2 r50 recipe from ImageNet (D96_PHASE=r50_gl, as v2), dual BN, one shared augmentation draw per study. (1) **No reader; hard routing:** finding f reads only the global windows (1e head) and its own regions (v2: own-crop attention 0.03 = chance, 44% attribution on other findings' crops). (2) **Gate:** fused_f = beta g_f + (1 - beta) l_f, beta = sigmoid(MLP([z_g,f ; z_l,f]) 4096-128-12, dropout 0.2, last layer trunc normal 0.02) -> starts at beta ~ 0.5 (v2: mean(g, l) - g +0.0047 fold 0 / +0.0034 training; v2's reader captured +0.0009); no detach. (3) **4 regions per finding in training and evaluation** (MS1's 4 windows / points, jitter +-16 px; learned per-finding attention over the 4) (v2: 1 crop in training vs 4 in evaluation, top-1 0.908 vs 4-crop 0.929; local plateaued at epoch ~11, train - fold 0 gap 0.043 vs global 0.034). (4) **Loss = BCE(fused) + BCE(global) + BCE(local)** (full weight each; with the gate these are the both / global-only / regions-only states, so no hiding rate). (5) **Regions for 8 findings:** ACL, MCL, menisci, medial / lateral OA, Baker's, contusion; none for effusion, synovitis, PF OA, fracture (fused = global). (6) **Baker's regions sagittal** (MP-0 follow-up: training-fold CV 0.889 sagittal vs 0.851 axial; each of MS1's 4 points mapped through DICOM geometry, `multiplane_points.npz` points4). Boxes as v2 (128 px medial meniscus / contusion, 192 others -> 384). CPU-tested (stub r50): region table (Baker's to sagittal, other findings unchanged), init beta 0.47-0.53, fused = beta mix exactly and = global without regions, hard routing (perturbing one finding's regions moves only that finding; global unchanged), loss -> parameter paths, predict / score / BN refresh. Logged every epoch: fused / global / local AUC + val BCE, gate beta mean / sd per finding, training-set gate beta. Stretch (not a gate): 0.955 fold 0 (~0.950 LB). (3) **Crop branch**: helps ACL / MCL / lateral OA (f − g up to +0.015); **hurts PF OA and fracture on every seed and placement** (global-only for those two: +0.0012…+0.0017 on 4 replicates); box ×1.0 is optimal (×0.67 −0.005, ×1.5 −0.003, both seeds), 4 crops > top-1 (−0.003 / −0.007); MS1's table beats self-placement +0.0022 [+0.0001, +0.0041] / +0.0030 [+0.0005, +0.0054] (Baker's +0.011 / +0.013, contusion +0.010, ACL +0.008). (4) Grad-CAM: **PF OA self crops miss the patellofemoral joint because the crop centre is the centroid of a two-mode map (patella + posterior)**; Baker's global CAM sits on condyles / joint in sagittal, never the popliteal fossa on axial; fracture FPs are marrow-oedema look-alikes in the crop CAM; a top-left-corner hot spot appears in most crop CAMs (padding artefact, unverified). (5) **Seed variance large**: s2026 + s2027 self-located average +0.0026 over the singles' mean (logit corr 0.92–0.98, lowest MCL / fracture). (6) Labels: 75% of lateral OA and 56% of MCL positives are soft (0.7–0.85). (7) MS1-free r1self: MCL coronal attention 37% → 28%, fracture 34% → 26%. (8) **Peak-vs-centroid crop centre (kernel v2, `kaggle_xai_v2/`): peak is worse** — s2026 −0.0021 [−0.0036, −0.0006], s2027 −0.0010, r1self −0.0007; PF OA −0.006 / −0.004 (centres move ~60 px). The two-mode PF map seen in the panels is not the population mechanism; the PF crop branch is weak wherever it is placed. Inference patch = PF OA + fracture global-only only: s2026 +0.0015 [+0.0004, +0.0027] (100%), s2027 +0.0013 [−0.0002, +0.0031] (94%), r1self +0.0005. (9) **Plane-aware global head withdrawn before coding (2026-10-19)** after checking it against the ledger: the per-plane head is 2c/2d, and every earlier test of more non-sagittal voice (2c, 2d, 4b) traded clear-band OA for mild-band MCL / effusion / Baker's; 2d lost 0.012 on the LB. **On the clear-band screen (LB tracker; `scripts/audit_l4copy_clear6.py`, fit LB = −0.687 + 1.671 × clear6) the v4 gap to E3 is OA compartments, not the 12-target deficit list:** v4 s2026 self clear6 0.9725 (fit 0.938, LB 0.939) vs E3 0.9764 (fit 0.945, LB 0.944): lateral OA −0.010, medial OA −0.007, ACL / PF OA −0.004, medial meniscus / effusion 0. Baker's / contusion / synovitis are outside the band. MS1 crop placement closes part of the OA gap (lateral OA +0.003 / +0.015 s2026 / s2027, medial OA +0.006 / +0.004), so **crop localisation of the tibiofemoral compartments is the LB-relevant lever**. Inference variants (fit LB): s2026 self + PF/fracture global-only clear6 0.9730 (0.939); s2026 MS1 + patch 0.9749 (0.942); s2026+s2027 self seed mean + patch 0.9746 (0.942). (10) **Where self placement differs from MS1 (tables.npz vs the MS1 cp table, s2026):** within sagittal the slices agree (median 1–1.5 slices apart; medial vs lateral OA crops are 9 slices apart for self, 11 for MS1, so there is no wrong-compartment error). **The plane/sequence mix differs:** MS1 puts 24–29% of medial / lateral / PF OA crops on sagittal non-fluid (SagNF) against 2–3% for self; 85% of Baker's crops on axial against 0%; MCL coronal 84% vs 69%; contusion / fracture coronal 42–48% vs 21–42%. MS1 windows with self points give the best OA of any placement (clear band: medial OA 0.977, lateral OA 0.944 vs self 0.970 / 0.939). The crop branch was trained on MS1's mix, so the self locator feeds it a sequence mix it did not train on. Leads: (a) free inference test, OA crops forced partly onto SagNF at the matched slice; (b) a learned window/point locator distilled from MS1 placements at training time (user 2026-10-19: MS1 placements are OK, MS1 at inference is not), with a measured ceiling of the MS1-table fused path: 0.9475 / clear6 0.9733, and with the PF/fracture patch 0.9749 (fit 0.942). |

## Open decisions

- **Rule relaxation for #2 / #3: DECIDED 2026-10-10 by the user: relaxed, strictly out-of-fold.** Model
  predictions may shape *training* labels (cleaning, image-as-reader, distillation, pseudo-labels) only when
  produced by a model that never trained on that study or its duplicate/report group. Validation labels, the
  fold-0 ruler, any consensus ruler and gold are never altered by a model. Every label-set version is saved
  with its hash; each loop round must pass the ruler and the LB. No clinical-accuracy claims.
- **Gold in training:** recommended only in a final all-data run (#5). Not confirmed.
- **External weights (#4):** check the competition rules before using OrthoFoundation.
- **External data: user said yes if open (2026-10-10).** Competition rule 6: external data must be publicly
  available and equally accessible at no cost, or meet the Reasonableness standard. OAI (NIH Data Archive data-use
  certification) and fastMRI (NYU data-sharing agreement) are free but gated, not open in the strict sense:
  awaiting the user's call, plus a forum check for a host ruling. The public 0.949 CoAtNet credits OAI.
- **MeluXina allocation:** pending.

## Standing constraints

- Keep validation study IDs fixed for matched fold 0 comparisons, and keep a fixed Steven reference for cross-experiment audits. Validation-control label changes are allowed when explicitly part of the experiment: HPO train/validation has already been authorized and run. Record both label hashes, masks, controls and audit metrics; do not change a running experiment's reference or compare differently labelled AUCs as one scale.
- Current report-trained models exclude gold. Gold is development evidence, not a pristine repeated-test holdout; earlier 29-study fine-tunes are archived exceptions. Gold-inclusive final training is a separate open decision.
- No host GPU; GPU work runs on Kaggle or Modal (personal profile).
- No dependencies installed on the host; local checks run in containers with `--network=none`.
- Reports and DICOM never go to external services.
- Fixed input paths in Kaggle notebooks, no recursive search.
- Pre-register acceptance criteria before each run; report every target, including unfavourable ones.

## Review / provenance (2026-10-10)

Reviewed against saved configs, run summaries, checkpoint-export receipt, audit scripts and official account submission history. Main corrections: tied 0.925 336 px single model; completed HPO-validation run; manual 24-epoch r34 stop; CoAtNet result and partial RadImageNet history; separate pairwise vs saddle objectives; gold fine-tuning history; prior AUC-control and sequence/slice-series results; fixed-FOV vs fit-mode distinction; narrower conclusions from failed recipes. Review receipts and a copy of the pre-review ledger are in `data/experiment_ledger_review_2026-10-10/` (gitignored). Live TTA status was not independently queried, and external paper/model/licence claims were not revalidated in this local review.

**Follow-up correction:** inspection of `SpatialAttentionPool` confirmed that the teammate already implemented the proposed self-attention over spatial feature-map positions plus target-query pooling. It has been moved from an untried proposal to an existing experiment requiring attribution/learning diagnostics. The 0.8849 attention-only run instead used same-series attention over window vectors.
| KD-GAP | **Why no student matched its teacher: ablation + XAI + Grad-CAM (CPU + free Kaggle T4; no training, no submission; training paused).** User 2026-10-20: "lets pause training right now, we have trained a lot of models and didn't get any model that matched or surpassed the teachers, I want you to do a detailed ablation analysis, XAI and gradcam to understand why". Gap: Raptor 384 single 0.949 (nartaa; our port 0.949) / full blend 0.958 vs r50 l4copy students ra 0.943, B 0.946, E4 0.946 (v4 no-KD 0.939). Teacher in-sample on fold 0; the fastMRI holdout (500 patients, no labels) is the clean copy measure. **CPU part** (`scripts/audit_kd_gap.py` -> `data/coatnet_d96_fold0/kd_gap_analysis/kd_gap_cpu.txt`): (S1) ra train KL to its targets 0.0122 = fold-0 KL 0.0125: no overfitting, the student cannot fit even its training targets (floor); (S2) clean fastMRI fidelity ra 0.947 = the teacher's own 224-vs-384 agreement 0.948, F1 (+fastMRI) 0.970, yet the 224 sibling scores 0.945 and F1 is predicted only +0.002: copying to ~0.95-0.97 is not enough, the teacher's edge sits in the residual; (S3) no shrinkage (slopes 1.0-1.16), errors largest at teacher p 0.3-0.7; (S4) crop branch adds <= +0.001 under KD (rap2: global > fused); (S5) train loss and lv2 AUC still moving at epoch 25; (S6) E4 closes 0% of the v4->teacher gap on ACL, PF OA, fracture, ~50% on Medial OA / Baker's. **Existing KD-R XAI kernel output analysed** (`xai_kdr/xai_kdr_compare.txt`): teacher spreads window attention over SagF / SagNF / Ax (Ax 13-37%, Baker's 37%); students 50-80% SagF, Ax 1-13%; KD moved slot mass only part way (L1 to teacher 0.315 -> 0.217); students' attention flatter than the teacher's on positives (entropy pos-neg gap 0.03 vs 0.07). **Mechanism hypothesis (pre-registered)**: view inconsistency. Targets = teacher on all 94 windows, 320 centre crop, no augmentation; students train on 16 stratified random windows of the whole study (centres(mask, 16, train=True)), whole 384 field with mirror / affine / gamma, so focal evidence (ACL, fracture, meniscus) is often absent from the training view while the target still says positive (Beyer et al. 2022: KD needs a consistent and patient teacher). **GPU part** (`scripts/build_kaggle_kd_gap_kernel.py` -> kernel `tumis001/rsna-kd-gap-xai`, `kaggle/submission_kernels/kd_gap_xai/`): part 1 fold 0, teacher + v4 / ra / B / E4: window attention, series ablation (drop / only slot), gradient x activation maps on the teacher's top window and each model's own, 14 teacher-right / E4-wrong panels; part 2, 200 training studies: teacher on its target view vs 16-window draws with / without augmentation, 94 windows + augmentation, whole field, the teacher-attention mass a 16-window draw covers, students on the clean training view. Decision rule: if the teacher's own KL from target view to student training view >= the students' train KL floor (~0.012 on ra) -> the floor is the view mismatch, next lever = consistent targets (teacher scored on the student's own sampled view, online or cached per draw) + longer schedule; if teacher reliance (series ablation) differs from the students' -> attention / series transfer; if CAMs disagree on the same window -> in-plane reading (resolution / capacity). |
| KD-GAP readout | **GPU part done (kernel `tumis001/rsna-kd-gap-xai` v1, T4, 73 min; teacher head check 0.0005; scored by `scripts/audit_kd_gap_xai.py` -> `data/coatnet_d96_fold0/kd_gap_analysis/kd_gap_xai/kd_gap_xai.txt`).** (X1 view consistency, 200 training studies) the teacher disagrees with ITSELF between its target view and the student training view by KL 0.0285 nats/cell (16 windows + aug; Spearman 0.907) = 2.3x ra's train-KL floor 0.0122 -> pre-registered rule MET: the floor is the view mismatch. Split: 16 windows alone 0.0112, 94 windows + aug 0.0177, whole field (no crop) 0.0073: augmentation is the larger part, largest on Medial / Lateral meniscus and OA (0.04-0.05). A 16-window draw covers 0.49 of the teacher's attention mass; 9% of teacher positives fall below 0.5 on a draw; mean of 4 draws Spearman 0.987 (targets right on average, wrong per sample). Students barely fit training studies better than fold 0 (ra 0.959 vs 0.944, E4 0.932 vs 0.922): underfit, not overfit. (X2 window attention, fold 0) JS to the teacher ra 0.117 < E4 0.147 < B 0.173 < v4 0.192, same order as fidelity; top-1 window within +-1 only 33-38%. Teacher top window on Ax for Baker's 44%, synovitis 30%, effusion 29%, contusion 26%, fracture 24%; on CorF for MCL 81%, fracture 52%, contusion 35%, medial OA 26%, medial meniscus 20%. E4 top window: Ax 0% for every finding, SagF 70-97% except MCL. (X3 series) dropping Ax: teacher |dlogit| 0.10-0.19 on Baker's / PF OA / effusion / contusion, AUC -0.0034 (in-sample); students |dlogit| 0.01-0.08, AUC ~0: students do not read axial at all. Students lean 2-4x harder on SagF (v4 ACL 1.25 vs teacher 0.31). (X4 Grad-CAM on the teacher's top window) low agreement for every student (Spearman 0.13 all / 0.22 positives, peak within 1 cell 0.28-0.30, centroid 50 px of 320) and flat across students (v4 0.125, ra 0.144) although fidelity spans 0.880-0.944; no CAM baseline (teacher 384 vs 224) yet, so cross-architecture CAM agreement is uninterpretable on its own. (X5) E4's percentile shortfall vs the teacher on Steven-confident positives tracks the window-attention JS (mean rho 0.155; contusion 0.31, MCL 0.25, lateral meniscus 0.23, ACL 0.20) but not CAM disagreement (0.03). Panels: Baker's 02602285 same posterior hot spot in both CAMs on the same SagF slice, teacher p 0.46 vs E4 0.16. **Verdict**: the gap is WHICH slices / series the student reads, not where it looks inside a slice; the training view (random 16 windows + augmentation, whole field) gives per-sample targets that disagree with the input as much as 2.3x the residual, and axial / coronal evidence the teacher uses is never learned. **Next (not launched; needs go)**: free T4 diagnostic first: decompose the augmentation KL (mirror / geometric / intensity), teacher-guided window draws (sample ∝ teacher attention mixed with uniform) coverage + KL vs random, CAM baseline teacher 384 vs 224. Then KD-CV = E4 recipe + guided windows + only target-consistent augmentation + window-attention distillation (KL to cached teacher attention) + longer schedule; gate on the neutral ruler vs Raptor 0.949. Caveat: fidelity to Raptor 384 is not LB by itself (ra most faithful, LB 0.943 < E4 0.946; S7 +0.023 fidelity -> +0.0017). |
| KD-VIEW | **Which student training view keeps the teacher's answer and the evidence? (free Kaggle 2x T4, fold 0, no training, no submission.)** User 2026-10-20 after KD-GAP: "isnt the solution to let the students learn on more slices ?" / "we dont want to match the teachers 100% we want to surpass them" / "yes". Kernel `tumis001/rsna-kd-view-sweep` (`scripts/build_kaggle_kd_view_sweep.py`), scored by `scripts/audit_kd_view_sweep.py`. Per fold-0 study, Raptor 384 teacher (all-data, in-sample) and E4 (out of fold) on 20 views: slice draws u94 / random 16, 32, 48 / teacher-guided 16, 32 (p = 0.5 teacher attention + 0.5 uniform) x augmentation none / mirror / fov (whole field) / geom / int / full (runner) / light (crop, half geometry, ~0.4x intensity, no mirror); attention mass covered per draw; Grad-CAM baseline = the 224 sibling on the 384's top window. Student-student CAM agreement already measured from KD-GAP part 1: ra-E4 0.58, B-E4 0.54, v4-ra 0.47 vs teacher-student 0.13-0.14 (v4 0.125 no KD): the in-plane map is set by the architecture and KD does not move it. Test (6 studies): 2 GPUs, ~18 s/study/GPU, 384 top windows identical to KD-GAP. **Pre-registered rules**: (R1 slices) the student view = the fewest windows (random or guided) with E4 dAUC (Steven-confident, vs E4 clean) >= -0.002 and teacher KL to clean <= 0.006 (half ra's floor); (R2 augmentation) a component is label-preserving if both teacher and E4 dAUC >= -0.002: keep it as student noise, with targets scored on the same augmented view when its teacher KL > 0.006; a component with dAUC < -0.002 loses evidence: drop it; (R3 CAM) 224-vs-384 agreement <= 0.25 -> cross-model Grad-CAM agreement is no signal here, in-plane reading closed as the explanation; >= 0.45 -> the students read a slice differently from the teacher family -> resolution / architecture lever. The chosen view feeds KD-CV (full-blend targets + out-of-fold report labels, longer schedule; paid, needs go). |

| KD-ATT-T | **Free test before KD-ATT (window-attention distillation): does E4's shortfall vs its teacher sit in the studies where the teacher reads non-sagittal series?** User go 2026-10-21 ("do the free test"); CPU, no training, no submission. Reliance R[i,j] = teacher |logit change| when Ax, CorF, CorNF are dropped (summed); secondary = teacher attention mass on non-sagittal windows. Cells = Steven-confident (fold-0 validation labels, unchanged). Shortfall per cell = teacher percentile − student percentile (positives), reversed for negatives. Statistic: share_hi = share of the student's total shortfall that sits in the top reliance tercile (per finding, then mean; 1/3 = no concentration). **Control = Raptor 224** (the teacher's own sibling, same training data, same reading habits, LB 0.945 ≈ E4): removes the in-sample / volatile-study artefact. Drop KD-ATT if E4 share_hi ≤ 0.38, or the difference is < 0.02 with CI including 0. Otherwise inconclusive → next free step = T4 oracle test (re-pool E4's own window features with the teacher's attention). Descriptive: per-tercile AUC gap on Steven cells, ra / B as extra students, secondary reliance.
